## How to investigate

Read the highest-value sources first:
- `README*`, root manifests, workspace config, lockfiles
- build, test, lint, formatter, typecheck, and codegen config
- CI workflows and pre-commit / task runner config
- existing instruction files (`AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.cursor/rules/`, `.cursorrules`, `.github/copilot-instructions.md`)
- repo-local OpenCode config such as `opencode.json`

If architecture is still unclear after reading config and docs, inspect a small number of representative code files to find the real entrypoints, package boundaries, and execution flow. Prefer reading the files that explain how the system is wired together over random leaf files.

Prefer executable sources of truth over prose. If docs conflict with config or scripts, trust the executable source and only keep what you can verify.

## What to extract

Look for the highest-signal facts for an agent working in this repo:
- exact developer commands, especially non-obvious ones
- how to run a single test, a single package, or a focused verification step
- required command order when it matters, such as `lint -> typecheck -> test`
- monorepo or multi-package boundaries, ownership of major directories, and the real app/library entrypoints
- framework or toolchain quirks: generated code, migrations, codegen, build artifacts, special env loading, dev servers, infra deploy flow
- repo-specific style or workflow conventions that differ from defaults
- testing quirks: fixtures, integration test prerequisites, snapshot workflows, required services, flaky or expensive suites
- important constraints from existing instruction files worth preserving

Good `AGENTS.md` content is usually hard-earned context that took reading multiple files to infer.

## Questions

Only ask the user questions if the repo cannot answer something important. Use the `question` tool for one short batch at most.

Good questions:
- undocumented team conventions
- branch / PR / release expectations
- missing setup or test prerequisites that are known but not written down

Do not ask about anything the repo already makes clear.

## Writing rules

Include only high-signal, repo-specific guidance such as:
- exact commands and shortcuts the agent would otherwise guess wrong
- architecture notes that are not obvious from filenames
- conventions that differ from language or framework defaults
- setup requirements, environment quirks, and operational gotchas
- references to existing instruction sources that matter

Exclude:
- generic software advice
- long tutorials or exhaustive file trees
- obvious language conventions
- speculative claims or anything you could not verify
- content better stored in another file referenced via `opencode.json` `instructions`

When in doubt, omit.

Prefer short sections and bullets. If the repo is simple, keep the file simple. If the repo is large, summarize the few structural facts that actually change how an agent should work.

If `AGENTS.md` already exists at `/home/bit/new/codex.1`, improve it in place rather than rewriting blindly. Preserve verified useful guidance, delete fluff or stale claims, and reconcile it with the current codebase.

1: # 电动汽车充电桩应用管理平台
2: 
3: 当前课程设计版本：**V0.8.0**。
4: 
5: V0.8.0 新增充电桩边缘服务器（由原设备模拟器增强而来）。边缘端自带 SQLite，保存充电桩、订单和遥测信息；客户端开始/停止充电均经过中心服务器转发，只有客户端、中心服务器、充电桩三方 TLS 连接正常且充电桩确认落单后，中心订单才会启动。中心网络中断时边缘端继续本地计量，重连后自动同步未完成/待结算订单，并以充电桩端状态为准修正中心状态。
6: 
7: 按金额、按电量、按时间三种模式均在启动前检查余额。其中按时间目标的单位为分钟，按“目标分钟 ÷ 60 × 电桩额定最大功率 × 电站单价”预估最大费用；达到任一模式目标后，充电桩本地自动停止并请求中心结算。
8: 
9: V0.7.0 新增管理员电站编辑/删除、电桩新增/编辑/删除、真实电桩数量自动汇总、固定数据库路径与旧数据库自动迁移，并统一管理端表格为随窗口宽度自适应填满。统计表格已设置为只读，所有资料修改均通过 TLS 请求写入 SQLite。
10: 
11: 第一阶段包含服务器、Qt Widgets 桌面用户端、Qt Quick/QML Android 手机端、管理端和设备模拟器。桌面固定界面保存在 `.ui` 文件中；手机端位于 `mobile_client`，采用适合触摸、自适应布局和 Android 打包的 QML。Web 大屏和机器学习不在本阶段。
12: 
13: 管理端已包含运营总览、近 7 天营收折线图、电桩状态环形图、站点营收排行，以及电站、电桩、订单、用户、审计日志五个数据页面。可新增电站、冻结/解冻用户，并可重启非充电状态的模拟电桩。
14: 
15: V0.6 完成安全认证与移动端导航：全部客户端、管理端和设备模拟器均改用 TLS 1.2+；用户密码仅在 TLS 通道中传输，数据库保存随机盐和 50000 轮 SHA-256 派生哈希。用户注册、登录和充值均执行前后端校验，登录/充值连续 5 次密码错误会锁定账户，管理员可在用户管理页解锁。手机端提供“首页 / 找桩 / 充电 / 我的”四个可跳转页面，站点和订单列表支持滚动。管理员登录后可安全创建其他管理员账号，禁止匿名注册管理员。
16: 
17: ## Linux 构建
18: 
19: 安装 Qt 5 Core、Widgets、Network、SQL、Charts 和 SQLite 驱动后执行：
20: 
21: ```bash
22: cd charging-platform
23: qmake charging-platform.pro
24: make -j"$(nproc)"
25: ```
26: 
27: ## 运行
28: 
29: ```bash
30: cp config/app.ini.example config/app.ini
31: ./server/ev_server --config config/app.ini
32: ./device_simulator/ev_device_simulator --code DL-SW-001 \
33:   --database data/charger-edge.db --token course-device-token
34: ./user_client/ev_user_client
35: ./admin_client/ev_admin_client
36: ```
37: 
38: 管理员初始账号为 `admin`，密码为 `123456`。首次成功登录会把旧版 SHA-256 哈希自动升级为加盐迭代哈希。腾讯地图 Key 填入 `config/app.ini` 的 `map/api_key`，不得提交真实 Key。
39: 
40: TLS 演示证书和私钥位于 `config`。课程验收可直接使用；公开部署必须更换私钥和受信任证书，详见 `SECURITY.md`。
41: 
42: 中心端 `config/app.ini` 的 `device/token` 必须与充电桩端 `--token` 一致。可以用英文逗号一次模拟多个设备，例如 `--code DL-SW-001,DL-SW-002`。详细协议、数据库和断线同步流程见 `docs/CHARGER_EDGE.md`。
43: 
44: 如需立即看到订单、用户和营收图表，请先停止服务器，再导入演示数据：
45: 
46: ```bash
47: sqlite3 data/charging.db < database/test_data.sql
48: ```

1: # Linux 部署说明
2: 
3: ## 环境要求
4: 
5: - Ubuntu 22.04 或更高版本
6: - Qt 5，包含 Core、Widgets、Network、SQL、Charts、QML、Quick Controls 2
7: - SQLite Qt 驱动
8: - GNU C++ 编译器和 make
9: 
10: Ubuntu 可使用以下命令安装常用依赖：
11: 
12: ```bash
13: sudo apt update
14: sudo apt install build-essential qt5-qmake qtbase5-dev libqt5charts5-dev \
15:   libqt5sql5-sqlite sqlite3 qtdeclarative5-dev qtquickcontrols2-5-dev \
16:   qml-module-qtquick2 qml-module-qtquick-controls2 \
17:   qml-module-qtquick-layouts qml-module-qtquick-window2
18: ```
19: 
20: ## 编译
21: 
22: ```bash
23: cd charging-platform
24: mkdir -p build
25: cd build
26: qmake ../charging-platform.pro
27: make -j"$(nproc)"
28: ```
29: 
30: ## 配置
31: 
32: ```bash
33: cd ..
34: cp config/app.ini.example config/app.ini
35: ```
36: 
37: 编辑 `config/app.ini`。腾讯地图 Key 只填写在本地配置文件中，不提交到代码仓库。数据库路径的父目录必须可写。
38: 
39: ## 启动顺序
40: 
41: ```bash
42: ./build/server/ev_server --config config/app.ini
43: ./build/device_simulator/ev_device_simulator --code DL-SW-001 \
44:   --database data/charger-edge.db --token course-device-token
45: ./build/user_client/ev_user_client
46: ./build/mobile_client/ev_mobile_client
47: ./build/admin_client/ev_admin_client
48: ```
49: 
50: 管理员初始账号为 `admin`，密码为 `123456`。首次完整演示前应修改初始密码。
51: 
52: ## 测试数据
53: 
54: 服务器首次启动会自动创建基础站点、电桩、电价和管理员。需要附加演示数据时，在停止服务器后执行：
55: 
56: ```bash
57: sqlite3 data/charging.db < database/test_data.sql
58: ```
59: 
60: 测试数据包含正常用户、冻结用户、已完成订单、钱包流水和已解决告警。
61: 
62: ## 常见问题
63: 
64: - 提示 `QSQLITE driver not loaded`：安装 `libqt5sql5-sqlite` 并重新启动。
65: - 客户端无法连接：确认服务器已启动，端口与 `config/app.ini` 一致，且防火墙允许该端口。
66: - 提示充电桩与服务器连接断开：确认充电桩端进程正在运行、`--code` 与中心数据库电桩编号一致，且 `--token` 与 `device/token` 一致。
67: - 地图不可用：检查 `map/api_key`，并确认虚拟机能够联网。
68: - 中文显示异常：安装常用中文字体，例如 `fonts-noto-cjk`。

(End of file - total 68 lines)
</content>
```

<content>
1: # 安全设计说明
2: 
3: ## 通信
4: 
5: 服务器仅接受 TLS 1.2 或更高版本连接。桌面用户端、手机端、管理员端和设备模拟器使用 `QSslSocket`，并信任项目内置的课程服务器证书。因此 JSON 帧、密码、订单和余额不会以明文经过网络。
6: 
7: Windows Qt 5.15.2 运行 TLS 时需要匹配架构的 OpenSSL 1.1.x 运行库。若服务器报告 `TLS initialization failed`，请通过 Qt 安装维护工具安装 OpenSSL 运行组件，或把官方匹配的 `libssl-1_1-x64.dll` 与 `libcrypto-1_1-x64.dll` 放到可执行文件目录。OpenSSL 3.x DLL 不能替代它们。
8: 
9: `config/server-cert.pem` 和 `config/server-key.pem` 是仅供课程局域网演示的证书。正式部署时必须更换，并只允许服务器部署人员读取私钥；客户端仅分发公钥证书。
10: 
11: ## 密码
12: 
13: - 客户端不保存密码，也不记录密码日志。
14: - TLS 负责传输机密性和服务器身份校验；没有采用“客户端先哈希再发送”，因为固定哈希会成为可重放的等价密码。
15: - 服务端为每个账户生成 128 位随机盐，并使用 Qt 自带 `QCryptographicHash` 进行 50000 轮 SHA-256 派生。
16: - 哈希比较采用常量时间比较。
17: - 登录和充值共享失败次数；成功验证后清零，累计连续失败 5 次后状态变为 `LOCKED`。
18: - 管理员用户管理页的“解锁 / 解冻”会恢复 `NORMAL` 并清零失败次数。
19: 
20: ## 数据库迁移
21: 
22: 服务端启动时会自动为旧 SQLite 数据库增加 `password_salt`、`password_hash`、`failed_attempts` 和 `locked_at` 等字段。旧测试用户没有密码哈希，可以通过注册流程为原手机号补设密码。旧管理员第一次成功登录后自动升级哈希。

(End of file - total 22 lines)
</content>
```

1: # 使用 VS Code 构建和预览
2: 
3: VS Code 可以在 Windows 直接编译并弹出 Qt 页面，但 VS Code 本身不包含 Qt。需要先安装 Qt 5 桌面 MinGW 工具链，并确保安装 Qt Charts、Qt Quick/QML、Qt Quick Controls 2 和对应的 MinGW 编译器。
4: 
5: ## Windows 一次性设置
6: 
7: 1. 安装 Qt 5，并选择 Desktop MinGW 64-bit、Qt Charts、Qt Quick Controls 2、Qt 的 MinGW Tools 和 OpenSSL 1.1.x 64-bit 运行库。
8: 2. 在 VS Code 安装 `Qt Extension for VS Code` 和 `C/C++` 扩展。
9: 3. 将项目中的 `charging-platform` 文件夹作为工作区根目录打开。
10: 4. 确保 VS Code 终端可以执行 `qmake -v` 和 `mingw32-make -v`。
11: 
12: 如果命令找不到，可以从 Qt 自带的 MinGW 命令行启动 VS Code，或者在 PowerShell 临时加入 PATH。以下路径只是示例，应替换为本机实际版本：
13: 
14: ```powershell
15: $env:Path="D:\QtSDK\5.15.2\mingw81_64\bin;D:\QtSDK\Tools\mingw810_64\bin;$env:Path"
16: qmake -v
17: mingw32-make -v
18: code .
19: ```
20: 
21: ## 一键构建
22: 
23: 项目已提供 `.vscode/tasks.json`：
24: 
25: - 按 `Ctrl+Shift+B` 执行 `Qt: Build`。
26: - 按 `Ctrl+Shift+P`，选择 `Tasks: Run Task`。
27: - 运行 `Run: Server` 启动服务器。
28: - 运行 `Run: Device Simulator` 启动设备模拟器。
29: - 再运行 `Run: Admin` 查看运营管理端。
30: - 运行 `Run: Desktop User` 查看 Qt Widgets 用户端。
31: - 运行 `Run: Mobile Preview` 查看 QML 手机端。
32: 
33: Windows 构建目录为 `build-vscode`。数据库相对于服务器启动目录创建在 `build-vscode/data/charging.db`。
34: 
35: ## QML 实时预览
36: 
37: Qt 官方 VS Code 扩展支持 QML Preview。先成功构建 `mobile_client`，然后在命令面板执行 `Qt: Preview changes to QML code live in your application`，选择 `ev_mobile_client.exe`。若扩展找不到资源文件，把 `build-vscode` 加入 Qt QML Preview 的 Additional Build Dirs。
38: 
39: ## 常见问题
40: 
41: - `qmake 不是内部或外部命令`：Qt 的 `bin` 没有加入 PATH。
42: - `mingw32-make 不是内部或外部命令`：Qt Tools 中 MinGW 的 `bin` 没有加入 PATH。
43: - `cannot find -lQt5Charts`：当前 Qt Kit 未安装 Charts，或 qmake 与 MinGW 来自不同 Qt 安装。
44: - `Unknown module(s) in QT: quickcontrols2`：当前 Qt Kit 未安装 Qt Quick Controls 2。
45: - `TLS initialization failed` 或 `QSslSocket::supportsSsl() == false`：缺少与 Qt 5.15.2 匹配的 OpenSSL 1.1.x 64-bit DLL；不要使用 OpenSSL 3.x DLL冒充。
46: - 服务器提示端口占用：Windows 和虚拟机不要同时启动占用同一个映射端口的服务器。

(End of file - total 46 lines)
</content>
```

<content>
1: # Android 手机端构建说明
2: 
3: `mobile_client` 是独立的 Qt Quick/QML 手机客户端，包含服务器连接、手机号登录、余额充值、站点选择、20 分钟预约、开始/停止充电和用户订单列表。Android 清单已声明网络权限并锁定竖屏。
4: 
5: ## Linux 桌面预览
6: 
7: 安装 Qt 5 QML 开发和运行模块：
8: 
9: ```bash
10: sudo apt install qtdeclarative5-dev qtquickcontrols2-5-dev \
11: qml-module-qtquick2 qml-module-qtquick-controls2 \
12: qml-module-qtquick-layouts qml-module-qtquick-window2
13: ```
14: 
15: 在项目构建目录重新执行：
16: 
17: ```bash
18: qmake ../charging-platform.pro
19: make -j"$(nproc)"
20: ./mobile_client/ev_mobile_client
21: ```
22: 
23: 桌面预览连接本机服务器时使用 `127.0.0.1:9527`。
24: 
25: ## 生成 Android APK
26: 
27: 1. 在 Qt Maintenance Tool 中安装与桌面版本一致的 Qt 5 Android 组件。
28: 2. 安装 Android SDK、NDK 和 JDK，并在 Qt Creator 的“设备/Android”页面完成检测。
29: 3. 在 Qt Creator 中打开 `mobile_client/mobile_client.pro`。
30: 4. 选择 Android Qt 5 Kit，而不是 Desktop Kit。
31: 5. 使用 Debug 构建并连接手机或启动模拟器，点击运行；发布时选择 Release 并配置签名证书。
32: 
33: 生成的 APK 通常位于 Android 构建目录的 `android-build/build/outputs/apk/` 下。
34: 
35: ## 真机服务器地址
36: 
37: Android 真机中的 `127.0.0.1` 指手机自身，不能连接电脑服务器。手机和服务器需要在同一局域网，在手机端“我的 → 服务器设置”填写服务器电脑的局域网 IP，例如 `192.168.1.105`，端口为 `9527`。
38: 
39: 服务器侧需要监听所有网卡并允许端口通过防火墙：
40: 
41: ```bash
42: sudo ufw allow 9527/tcp
43: ```
44: 
45: 如果服务器运行在虚拟机内，还需要将虚拟机网络设为桥接模式，或配置 NAT 端口转发。

**Output:**
```
<path>/home/bit/new/codex.1/docs/CHARGER_EDGE.md</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: # 充电桩边缘服务器设计
2: 
3: ## 链路与安全
4: 
5: 客户端不直接连接充电桩。所有业务均沿以下链路执行：
6: 
7: ```text
8: Qt 桌面/手机客户端 --TLS 1.2+--> 中心服务器 --TLS 1.2+--> 充电桩边缘服务器
9: ```
10: 
11: 中心服务器和充电桩使用与现有客户端相同的“4 字节大端长度 + JSON”帧格式和证书固定校验。充电桩首次连接发送 `device.register`，并用 `device/token` 共享凭据登记自己管理的电桩编号。真实部署应为每个站点分配不同凭据，本课程版本使用一个配置项便于演示。
12: 
13: ## 本地 SQLite
14: 
15: 充电桩端默认数据库为 `data/charger-edge.db`，包含：
16: 
17: - `chargers`：编号、类型、额定功率、当前状态、累计次数和时长；
18: - `charge_orders`：中心订单号、用户、模式、目标、起止时间、电量、时长、金额和同步状态；
19: - `telemetry`：电压、电流、功率、SOC 和累计电量采样。
20: 
21: 本地订单主要状态：
22: 
23: - `CHARGING`：本地持续计量，即使中心连接中断也不停止；
24: - `SYNC_PENDING`：已经在充电桩停止，等待中心服务器结算确认；
25: - `COMPLETED`：中心结算成功，双方状态一致；
26: - `CANCELLED`：中心启动失败后撤销。
27: 
28: ## 开始充电
29: 
30: 1. 客户端经 TLS 登录并发送 `charge.start`。
31: 2. 中心服务器校验用户、余额、模式、目标和电桩状态，先建立 `STARTING` 订单。
32: 3. 中心服务器确认该电桩存在已登记的 TLS 会话，然后发送 `device.order.start`。
33: 4. 充电桩把订单写入本地 SQLite 后返回确认。
34: 5. 中心服务器把订单改为 `CHARGING`，最后才向客户端返回成功。
35: 
36: 任何一方缺少连接或充电桩十秒内未确认，客户端都会收到失败结果，订单不会进入充电状态。
37: 
38: ## 计费模式
39: 
40: - `AMOUNT`：目标单位为元；余额至少等于目标金额。
41: - `ENERGY`：目标单位为 kWh；预计费用为目标电量乘电站单价。
42: - `TIME`：目标单位为分钟；最大预计费用为 `分钟 / 60 × 额定功率(kW) × 电站单价`。
43: 
44: 充电桩按本地计量数据判断目标。边界采样会裁剪到目标，避免因两秒采样步长造成余额校验后仍发生超额费用。
45: 
46: ## 停止、断线与重连
47: 
48: 客户端停止订单时，中心服务器必须能联系对应充电桩。连接断开时返回“充电桩与服务器连接断开，暂时无法停止订单”，充电桩仍继续本地计量。
49: 
50: 充电桩重连后自动执行 `device.register` 和 `device.sync`：
51: 
52: 1. 上报本地电桩状态，中心以本地状态为准；
53: 2. `CHARGING` 订单同步最新电量和时长；
54: 3. `SYNC_PENDING` 订单由中心扣款并生成钱包流水；
55: 4. 中心返回结算成功后，充电桩才把本地订单改为 `COMPLETED`。
56: 
57: 中心重启不会主动结束充电订单，客户端重新登录后会恢复当前活动订单号，可以在充电桩重连后继续停止。
58: 
59: ## 验证
60: 
61: 完成 Release 编译后执行：
62: 
63: ```powershell
64: python tests\test_edge_integration.py
65: ```
66: 
67: 该脚本只使用系统临时目录中的数据库，覆盖三方启动、按时间自动停止、设备离线拒绝启动、断线本地计量和重连结算。

(End of file - total 67 lines)
</content>
```

**Output:**

<path>/home/bit/new/codex.1/tests/test_edge_integration.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """End-to-end TLS test for client -> central server -> charger edge server.
2: 
3: Uses temporary SQLite files and never touches the developer database.
4: Run after a release build: python tests/test_edge_integration.py
5: """
6: from __future__ import annotations
7: 
8: import json
9: import os
10: import socket
11: import sqlite3
12: import ssl
13: import struct
14: import subprocess
15: import tempfile
16: import time
17: import uuid
18: from pathlib import Path
19: 
20: 
21: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
22: BUILD = ROOT / "build-vscode"
23: SERVER = BUILD / "server" / "release" / "ev_server.exe"
24: DEVICE = BUILD / "device_simulator" / "release" / "ev_device_simulator.exe"
25: CERT = ROOT / "config" / "server-cert.pem"
26: KEY = ROOT / "config" / "server-key.pem"
27: PORT = 19527
28: 
29: 
30: class Client:
31:     def __init__(self):
32:         context = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=str(CERT))
33:         raw = socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", PORT), timeout=5)
34:         self.sock = context.wrap_socket(raw, server_hostname="localhost")
35: 
36:     def request(self, kind: str, payload: dict, timeout: float = 12) -> dict:
37:         request_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
38:         message = {"version": 1, "type": kind, "requestId": request_id,
39:                    "timestamp": int(time.time() * 1000), "payload": payload}
40:         body = json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
41:         self.sock.sendall(struct.pack(">I", len(body)) + body)
42:         self.sock.settimeout(timeout)
43:         while True:
44:             size = struct.unpack(">I", self._read(4))[0]
45:             response = json.loads(self._read(size))
46:             if response.get("requestId") == request_id:
47:                 return response
48: 
49:     def _read(self, size: int) -> bytes:
50:         data = b""
51:         while len(data) < size:
52:             part = self.sock.recv(size - len(data))
53:             if not part:
54:                 raise ConnectionError("server closed the TLS connection")
55:             data += part
56:         return data
57: 
58:     def close(self):
59:         self.sock.close()
60: 
61: 
62: def wait_tls(deadline: float = 10):
63:     end = time.time() + deadline
64:     while time.time() < end:
65:         try:
66:             client = Client()
67:             return client
68:         except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
69:             time.sleep(0.25)
70:     raise RuntimeError("central TLS server did not become ready")
71: 
72: 
73: def assert_ok(response: dict) -> dict:
74:     assert response.get("code") == 0, response
75:     return response.get("data", {})
76: 
77: 
78: def main():
79:     assert SERVER.exists() and DEVICE.exists(), "build the project before running this test"
80:     runtime = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="charging-edge-test-"))
81:     central_db, edge_db = runtime / "central.db", runtime / "edge.db"
82:     config = runtime / "app.ini"
83:     config.write_text(
84:         f"[server]\nport={PORT}\n[tls]\ncertificate={CERT.as_posix()}\n"
85:         f"private_key={KEY.as_posix()}\n[database]\npath={central_db.as_posix()}\n"
86:         "[device]\ntoken=edge-test-token\n", encoding="utf-8")
87:     env = os.environ.copy()
88:     env["PATH"] = (r"D:\QtSDK\5.15.2\mingw81_64\bin;"
89:                    r"D:\QtSDK\Tools\mingw810_64\bin;"
90:                    r"D:\Anaconda\Library\bin;" + env.get("PATH", ""))
91:     env["QT_PLUGIN_PATH"] = r"D:\QtSDK\5.15.2\mingw81_64\plugins"
92:     flags = getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0)
93:     server = device = None
94:     log_server = (runtime / "server.log").open("w", encoding="utf-8")
95:     log_device = (runtime / "device.log").open("w", encoding="utf-8")
96:     phone = "139" + f"{int(time.time()) % 100_000_000:08d}"
97:     password = "EdgeTest123"
98:     try:
99:         server = subprocess.Popen([str(SERVER), "--config", str(config)], env=env,
100:                                   stdout=log_server, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
101:                                   creationflags=flags)
102:         bootstrap = wait_tls()
103:         device = subprocess.Popen([str(DEVICE), "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(PORT),
104:                                    "--code", "DL-SW-001", "--database", str(edge_db),
105:                                    "--token", "edge-test-token"], env=env,
106:                                   stdout=log_device, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
107:                                   creationflags=flags)
108:         time.sleep(2)
109:         assert_ok(bootstrap.request("auth.user.register", {"phone": phone, "password": password,
110:                                                             "confirmPassword": password}))
111:         assert_ok(bootstrap.request("wallet.recharge", {"amount": 200, "password": password}))
112: 
113:         # TIME means minutes. 0.05 minute completes locally after about four seconds.
114:         started = assert_ok(bootstrap.request("charge.start", {"chargerId": 1,
115:                                                                  "mode": "TIME", "target": 0.05}))
116:         order_id = int(started["orderId"])
117:         deadline = time.time() + 12
118:         status = None
119:         while time.time() < deadline:
120:             orders = assert_ok(bootstrap.request("user.orders", {}))["items"]
121:             status = next(x["status"] for x in orders if int(x["id"]) == order_id)
122:             if status == "COMPLETED":
123:                 break
124:             time.sleep(1)
125:         assert status == "COMPLETED", f"TIME order did not auto-complete: {status}"
126: 
127:         # Charger 2 has no connected edge process, so a three-party start must fail.
128:         offline = bootstrap.request("charge.start", {"chargerId": 2, "mode": "AMOUNT", "target": 5})
129:         assert offline.get("code") != 0 and "连接断开" in offline.get("message", ""), offline
130: 
131:         # Start a long order, interrupt the central server, then reconnect and settle it.
132:         active = assert_ok(bootstrap.request("charge.start", {"chargerId": 1,
133:                                                                 "mode": "AMOUNT", "target": 100}))
134:         active_id = int(active["orderId"])
135:         bootstrap.close();server.terminate();server.wait(timeout=5);server = None
136:         time.sleep(4)  # the edge process continues updating its own SQLite database
137:         server = subprocess.Popen([str(SERVER), "--config", str(config)], env=env,
138:                                   stdout=log_server, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
139:                                   creationflags=flags)
140:         client = wait_tls();time.sleep(4)
141:         assert_ok(client.request("auth.user", {"phone": phone, "password": password}))
142:         stopped = assert_ok(client.request("charge.stop", {"orderId": active_id}))
143:         assert stopped["status"] == "COMPLETED" and stopped["duration"] >= 4, stopped
144:         client.close();time.sleep(1)
145: 
146:         with sqlite3.connect(edge_db) as db:
147:             local_status = db.execute(
148:                 "SELECT status FROM charge_orders WHERE central_order_id=?", (active_id,)).fetchone()[0]
149:             assert local_status == "COMPLETED", local_status
150:         print("PASS: TLS three-party start, TIME maximum-power precheck/auto-stop, offline rejection,")
151:         print("      local SQLite metering during outage, reconnect reconciliation and settlement")
152:         print(f"Temporary test data: {runtime}")
153:     finally:
154:         for process in (device, server):
155:             if process and process.poll() is None:
156:                 process.terminate()
157:                 try: process.wait(timeout=5)
158:                 except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: process.kill()
159:         log_server.close();log_device.close()
160: 
161: 
162: if __name__ == "__main__":
163:     main()

1: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,phone TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,nickname TEXT NOT NULL,avatar_path TEXT,password_salt TEXT,password_hash TEXT,balance REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(balance>=0),status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',failed_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,locked_at TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL);
2: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admins(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,password_salt TEXT,password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ADMIN',status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',failed_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,locked_at TEXT);
3: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stations(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,name TEXT NOT NULL,address TEXT NOT NULL,longitude REAL NOT NULL,latitude REAL NOT NULL,base_price REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.2,status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ONLINE');
4: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chargers(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,station_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES stations(id),code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,type TEXT NOT NULL,rated_power REAL NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDLE',last_seen TEXT,total_sessions INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,total_duration INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
5: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reservations(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),charger_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES chargers(id),status TEXT NOT NULL,expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,created_at TEXT NOT NULL,cancel_reason TEXT);
6: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS charge_orders(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),charger_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES chargers(id),status TEXT NOT NULL,mode TEXT NOT NULL,target REAL NOT NULL,start_at TEXT NOT NULL,end_at TEXT,energy REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,duration INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,amount REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
7: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telemetry(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,charger_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES chargers(id),order_id INTEGER REFERENCES charge_orders(id),sampled_at TEXT NOT NULL,voltage REAL,current REAL,power REAL,soc REAL,temperature REAL,energy_total REAL);
8: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wallet_transactions(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),type TEXT NOT NULL,amount REAL NOT NULL,balance_after REAL NOT NULL,related_order_id INTEGER REFERENCES charge_orders(id),created_at TEXT NOT NULL);
9: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tariffs(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,name TEXT NOT NULL,start_time TEXT NOT NULL,end_time TEXT NOT NULL,energy_price REAL NOT NULL,service_price REAL NOT NULL,occupancy_price REAL NOT NULL,enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1);
10: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS alarms(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,charger_id INTEGER REFERENCES chargers(id),level TEXT NOT NULL,type TEXT NOT NULL,message TEXT NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN',created_at TEXT NOT NULL,resolved_at TEXT);
11: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operation_logs(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,actor_type TEXT,actor_id INTEGER,action TEXT,target_type TEXT,target_id INTEGER,result TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL);
12: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_user_status ON charge_orders(user_id,status);

1: #pragma once
2: 
3: #include <QFile>
4: #include <QSslCertificate>
5: #include <QSslConfiguration>
6: #include <QSslSocket>
7: 
8: namespace SecureConnect {
9: 
10: inline bool configure(QSslSocket *socket, QString *error = nullptr)
11: {
12:     QFile file(QStringLiteral(":/tls/server-cert.pem"));
13:     if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) {
14:         if (error) *error = QStringLiteral("无法读取内置服务器证书");
15:         return false;
16:     }
17:     const QSslCertificate certificate(file.readAll(), QSsl::Pem);
18:     if (certificate.isNull()) {
19:         if (error) *error = QStringLiteral("内置服务器证书无效");
20:         return false;
21:     }
22:     QSslConfiguration configuration = socket->sslConfiguration();
23:     QList<QSslCertificate> authorities = configuration.caCertificates();
24:     authorities.append(certificate);
25:     configuration.setCaCertificates(authorities);
26:     configuration.setPeerVerifyMode(QSslSocket::VerifyPeer);
27:     configuration.setProtocol(QSsl::TlsV1_2OrLater);
28:     socket->setSslConfiguration(configuration);
29:     return true;
30: }
31: 
32: inline bool connectToServer(QSslSocket *socket, const QString &host, quint16 port,
33:                             QString *error = nullptr)
34: {
35:     if (!QSslSocket::supportsSsl()) {
36:         if (error) *error = QStringLiteral("当前环境缺少 TLS/OpenSSL 运行库");
37:         return false;
38:     }
39:     if (!configure(socket, error)) return false;
40:     // The course certificate is pinned in the application.  Keep hostname
41:     // verification stable when a phone connects through a changing LAN IP.
42:     socket->setPeerVerifyName(QStringLiteral("localhost"));
43:     socket->connectToHostEncrypted(host, port);
44:     return true;
45: }
46: 
47: }

10: namespace Protocol {
11: 
12: inline QByteArray encode(const QJsonObject &object)
13: {
14:     const QByteArray body = QJsonDocument(object).toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact);
15:     QByteArray frame(4, Qt::Uninitialized);
16:     qToBigEndian<quint32>(static_cast<quint32>(body.size()),
17:                           reinterpret_cast<uchar *>(frame.data()));
18:     frame.append(body);
19:     return frame;
20: }
21: 
22: inline QList<QJsonObject> decode(QByteArray &buffer, QString *error = nullptr)
23: {
24:     QList<QJsonObject> messages;
25:     constexpr quint32 MaxFrameSize = 1024 * 1024;
26:     while (buffer.size() >= 4) {
27:         const quint32 length = qFromBigEndian<quint32>(
28:             reinterpret_cast<const uchar *>(buffer.constData()));
29:         if (length == 0 || length > MaxFrameSize) {
30:             if (error) *error = QStringLiteral("invalid frame length");
31:             buffer.clear();
32:             break;
33:         }
34:         if (buffer.size() < static_cast<int>(length + 4)) break;
35:         const QByteArray body = buffer.mid(4, length);
36:         buffer.remove(0, length + 4);
37:         QJsonParseError parseError;
38:         const QJsonDocument document = QJsonDocument::fromJson(body, &parseError);
39:         if (parseError.error != QJsonParseError::NoError || !document.isObject()) {
40:             if (error) *error = parseError.errorString();
41:             continue;
42:         }
43:         messages.append(document.object());
44:     }
45:     return messages;
46: }
47: 
48: inline QJsonObject request(const QString &type, const QJsonObject &payload,
49:                            const QString &requestId)
50: {
51:     return {{QStringLiteral("version"), 1},
52:             {QStringLiteral("type"), type},
53:             {QStringLiteral("requestId"), requestId},
54:             {QStringLiteral("timestamp"), QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch()},
55:             {QStringLiteral("payload"), payload}};
56: }
57: 
58: inline QJsonObject response(const QJsonObject &request, int code,
59:                             const QString &message, const QJsonObject &data = {})
60: {
61:     return {{QStringLiteral("version"), 1},
62:             {QStringLiteral("type"), request.value(QStringLiteral("type")).toString() + QStringLiteral(".result")},
63:             {QStringLiteral("requestId"), request.value(QStringLiteral("requestId"))},

## Assistant (Plan · MiMo V2.5 Free · 37.5s)

_Thinking:_

Now I have a thorough understanding of the repository. Let me summarize the key findings:

**Project overview:** This is an EV (electric vehicle) charging platform - a Qt 5 course project (V0.8.0) with:
- Central TLS server (C++, Qt Core/Network/SQL)
- Qt Widgets desktop user client
- Qt Quick/QML Android mobile client
- Qt Widgets admin client
- Device simulator (charger edge server with local SQLite)
- Shared common code (TLS, frame protocol, password utils)

**Architecture:**
- qmake-based build (SUBDIRS template), single `.pro` at root
- All subprojects depend on `server`
- `common/` is included via `.pri` (not a separate subdir) - provides TLS cert pinning, JSON frame codec, password utils
- Custom TLS protocol: 4-byte big-endian length prefix + JSON, TLS 1.2+
- SQLite database with auto-migration on startup
- Client -> Central Server -> Charger Edge Server TLS chain (clients never connect directly to chargers)

**Build:**
```bash
qmake charging-platform.pro
make -j"$(nproc)"
```
Or with out-of-source: `mkdir build && cd build && qmake ../charging-platform.pro && make -j"$(nproc)"`

**Testing:**
- All tests are Python scripts in `tests/` - standalone scripts, NOT pytest-based
- Run with `python tests/test_<name>.py`
- Most tests are architecture/structure tests that read source code or test SQL schema directly
- `test_edge_integration.py` is the key integration test - requires a release build first, starts server + device sim, does TLS end-to-end
- Windows paths hardcoded in `test_edge_integration.py` (not Linux-ready)

**Key gotchas:**
- `config/app.ini` is gitignored - must be copied from `app.ini.example` before running
- `config/server-key.pem` is gitignored for production but exists in the repo for demo
- `data/` directory is gitignored (DB files)
- `device/token` in app.ini must match `--token` CLI arg for device simulator
- Database path in config is relative to config dir (resolved via cdUp in main.cpp)
- Tests in `tests/` run on temp DBs, not the developer DB
- The edge integration test has hardcoded Windows paths

  "content": "# AGENTS.md\n\n## Project\n\nQt 5 (C++/QML) EV charging platform — course project V0.8.0. Five binaries: central TLS server, Qt Widgets desktop user client, Qt Quick/QML Android mobile client, Qt Widgets admin client, and a charger edge device simulator. All share TLS and protocol code from `common/`.\n\n## Architecture\n\n- Build system: qmake SUBDIRS (`charging-platform.pro`). All subprojects `depends = server`.\n- `common/` is not a subdir — included via `common.pri` (provides framecodec, secureconnect, passwordutils, and TLS certs as a qrc resource).\n- Wire protocol: 4-byte big-endian length prefix + compact JSON, over TLS 1.2+. See `common/framecodec.h`.\n- Clients never connect to chargers directly. Chain: Client → Central Server → Charger Edge Server (all TLS).\n- Central DB: `data/charging.db` (SQLite). Edge DB: `data/charger-edge.db` (SQLite, per simulator instance).\n- Server auto-migrates old DBs on startup (adds security columns etc). See `server/main.cpp:28-38`.\n\n## Build\n\n```bash\nqmake charging-platform.pro\nmake -j\"$(nproc)\"\n```\n\nOut-of-source (as .vscode tasks.json does):\n```bash\nmkdir -p build-vscode && cd build-vscode\nqmake ../charging-platform.pro\nmake -j$(nproc)\n```\n\nBinaries land in `build-vscode/<subdir>/ev_*`.\n\n## Run\n\nMust copy config first (gitignored):\n```bash\ncp config/app.ini.example config/app.ini\n```\n\nStartup order — server first, then simulator, then clients:\n```bash\n./build-vscode/server/ev_server --config config/app.ini\n./build-vscode/device_simulator/ev_device_simulator --code DL-SW-001 \\\n  --database data/charger-edge.db --token course-device-token\n./build-vscode/user_client/ev_user_client\n./build-vscode/admin_client/ev_admin_client\n```\n\n- `device/token` in app.ini **must** match the `--token` CLI arg.\n- Admin defaults: `admin` / `123456`.\n- DB path in config is relative to the config dir's parent (resolved in `server/main.cpp:30`).\n\n## Tests\n\nAll tests are standalone Python scripts in `tests/` — **not** pytest. Run directly:\n```bash\npython tests/test_database.py\npython tests/test_security_features.py\npython tests/test_admin_features.py\npython tests/test_edge_architecture.py\npython tests/test_management_v070.py\npython tests/test_ui_files.py\n```\n\nMost are structure/schema checks against source code or temp SQLite DBs.\n\n**Integration test** (`test_edge_integration.py`) requires a completed release build first. It starts the server and device simulator as subprocesses, runs TLS end-to-end. **Warning**: contains hardcoded Windows Qt/MinGW paths — will fail on Linux without editing `env[\"PATH\"]` and `env[\"QT_PLUGIN_PATH\"]` in that file.\n\n## Key Gotchas\n\n- `config/app.ini`, `config/server-key.pem`, and `data/` are gitignored. The repo ships `app.ini.example` and demo certs for course use.\n- `config/server-cert.pem` is embedded as a qrc resource in clients (`common/tls.qrc`). Changing the server cert requires rebuilding all clients.\n- `.ui` files (Qt Designer XML) define fixed layouts for user_client and admin_client. Edit in Qt Designer, not by hand.\n- Mobile client (`mobile_client/`) uses QML. The Android manifest is in `mobile_client/android/`.\n- Password hashing: 50000-round SHA-256 with random salt, done server-side in `server/database.cpp`. Never send pre-hashed passwords.\n- Charge orders follow a three-party protocol: central server must confirm with charger edge before marking CHARGING. See `docs/CHARGER_EDGE.md`.\n"
}

39:     m_registered=false;m_heartbeat.stop();
40:     qWarning()<<"central server disconnected; local charging and metering continue";
41:     QTimer::singleShot(3000,this,[this]{QString error;SecureConnect::connectToServer(&m_socket,m_socket.property("host").toString(),static_cast<quint16>(m_socket.property("port").toUInt()),&error);if(!error.isEmpty())qWarning()<<error;});
42: }
43: 
44: void Simulator::send(const QJsonObject &message){if(m_socket.isEncrypted())m_socket.write(Protocol::encode(message));}
45: void Simulator::sendRequest(const QString &type,const QJsonObject &payload){send(Protocol::request(type,payload,QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces)));}
46: 
47: void Simulator::readMessages()
48: {
49:     m_buffer+=m_socket.readAll();QString error;
50:     for(const QJsonObject &message:Protocol::decode(m_buffer,&error))dispatch(message);
51:     if(!error.isEmpty()){qWarning()<<"protocol error"<<error;m_socket.disconnectFromHost();}
52: }
53: 
54: void Simulator::dispatch(const QJsonObject &message)
55: {
56:     const QString type=message.value("type").toString();const QJsonObject payload=message.value("payload").toObject();QString error;QJsonObject data;
57:     if(type=="device.register.result"){
58:         if(message.value("code").toInt()!=0){qWarning()<<"device registration rejected"<<message.value("message").toString();m_socket.disconnectFromHost();return;}
59:         m_registered=true;heartbeat();sendSync();return;
60:     }
61:     if(type=="device.sync.result"){
62:         if(message.value("code").toInt()!=0){qWarning()<<"sync rejected"<<message.value("message").toString();return;}
63:         for(const auto &value:message.value("data").toObject().value("results").toArray()){
64:             const QJsonObject item=value.toObject();if(item.value("code").toInt()==0&&item.value("status").toString()=="COMPLETED")m_database.settleOrder(item.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong(),&error);
65:         }
66:         return;
67:     }
68:     if(type=="device.order.start")data=m_database.startOrder(payload,&error);
69:     else if(type=="device.order.stop")data=m_database.stopOrder(payload.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong(),&error);
70:     else if(type=="device.order.settled"){
71:         if(m_database.settleOrder(payload.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong(),&error))data={{"settled",true}};
72:     }else if(type=="device.order.abort"){
73:         if(m_database.abortOrder(payload.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong(),&error))data={{"aborted",true}};
74:     }else return;
75:     send(Protocol::response(message,error.isEmpty()?0:400,error.isEmpty()?"ok":error,data));
76: }
77: 
78: void Simulator::heartbeat()
79: {
80:     if(!m_registered)return;
81:     for(const QString &code:m_codes){QString error;sendRequest("device.heartbeat",{{"chargerCode",code},{"status",m_database.chargerStatus(code,&error)}});}
82: }
83: 
84: void Simulator::telemetry()
85: {
86:     bool completed=false;
87:     for(const QString &code:m_codes){
88:         QString error;const QString status=m_database.chargerStatus(code,&error);if(status!="CHARGING")continue;
89:         const double voltage=380.0+QRandomGenerator::global()->bounded(500)/100.0;
90:         const double ratedPower=m_database.chargerRatedPower(code,&error);
91:         const double power=ratedPower*(0.85+QRandomGenerator::global()->bounded(151)/1000.0);
92:         const double current=power*1000.0/voltage;m_soc[code]=qMin(100.0,m_soc.value(code,35.0)+0.05);
93:         const QJsonObject order=m_database.tick(code,voltage,current,power,m_soc.value(code),&error);
94:         if(!error.isEmpty()){qWarning()<<error;continue;}
95:         if(m_registered)sendRequest("device.telemetry",{{"chargerCode",code},{"voltage",voltage},{"current",current},{"power",power},{"soc",m_soc.value(code)}});
96:         if(order.value("status").toString()=="SYNC_PENDING")completed=true;
97:     }
98:     if(completed&&m_registered)sendSync();
99: }
100: 
101: void Simulator::sendSync()
102: {
103:     if(!m_registered)return;QString error;const QJsonArray orders=m_database.pendingOrders(&error);
104:     if(error.isEmpty())sendRequest("device.sync",{{"orders",orders}});else qWarning()<<error;
105: }

45:         qCritical() << "Cannot open TLS certificate/private key" << certificatePath << privateKeyPath;

61: void ServerApp::acceptConnections()
62: {
63:     while (m_server.hasPendingConnections()) {
64:         QSslSocket *socket=qobject_cast<QSslSocket *>(m_server.nextPendingConnection()); if(!socket)continue; m_buffers.insert(socket,{});
65:         connect(socket,&QSslSocket::readyRead,this,&ServerApp::readClient);
66:         connect(socket,&QSslSocket::disconnected,this,&ServerApp::removeClient);
67:     }
68: }

70: void ServerApp::readClient()
71: {
72:     auto *socket=qobject_cast<QSslSocket *>(sender()); if(!socket)return;
73:     QByteArray &buffer=m_buffers[socket]; buffer.append(socket->readAll()); QString error;
74:     const auto messages=Protocol::decode(buffer,&error);
75:     if(!error.isEmpty()){send(socket,{{"type","protocol.error"},{"code",400},{"message",error}});socket->disconnectFromHost();return;}
76:     for(const auto &message:messages)dispatch(socket,message);
77: }

84:     for(const QString &code:codes)if(m_chargerSockets.value(code)==socket){m_chargerSockets.remove(code);disconnectedCodes.append(code);}
85:     QString ignored;if(!disconnectedCodes.isEmpty())m_database.markDeviceOffline(disconnectedCodes,&ignored);
86:     const qint64 userId=socket->property("userId").toLongLong();
87:     if(userId>0&&m_userSockets.value(userId)==socket)m_userSockets.remove(userId);
88:     m_buffers.remove(socket);socket->deleteLater();
89: }

107:                 QSslSocket *device=connectedDevice(pending.chargerCode);if(device)send(device,Protocol::request("device.order.abort",{{"orderId",pending.orderId}},QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces)));

109:             continue;
110:         }
111:         if(m_chargerSockets.value(pending.chargerCode,nullptr)==socket){
112:             m_pendingCommands.remove(key);
113:             QString ignored;if(pending.action=="start")m_database.cancelPendingCharge(pending.orderId,reason,&ignored);
114:             if(pending.client)send(pending.client,Protocol::response(pending.clientMessage,503,"充电桩与服务器连接断开"));
115:         }
116:     }
117: }

119: void ServerApp::handleDeviceResult(QSslSocket *socket,const QJsonObject &message)
120: {
121:     const QString requestId=message.value("requestId").toString();
122:     if(!m_pendingCommands.contains(requestId))return;
123:     const PendingCommand pending=m_pendingCommands.take(requestId);
124:     if(connectedDevice(pending.chargerCode)!=socket)return;
125:     const int code=message.value("code").toInt(500);
126:     const QJsonObject deviceData=message.value("data").toObject();
127:     QString error;QJsonObject result;
128:     if(code!=0){
129:         if(pending.action=="start")m_database.cancelPendingCharge(pending.orderId,message.value("message").toString(),&error);
130:         if(pending.client)send(pending.client,Protocol::response(pending.clientMessage,400,message.value("message").toString("充电桩拒绝执行")));
131:         return;
132:     }
133:     if(pending.action=="start"){
134:         if(!pending.client){m_database.cancelPendingCharge(pending.orderId,"客户端已断开",&error);send(socket,Protocol::request("device.order.abort",{{"orderId",pending.orderId}},QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces)));return;}
135:         result=m_database.activatePendingCharge(pending.userId,pending.orderId,&error);
136:     }else if(pending.action=="stop"){
137:         result=m_database.completeChargeFromDevice(pending.orderId,deviceData.value("energy").toDouble(),deviceData.value("duration").toInt(),deviceData.value("endAt").toString(),&error);
138:         if(error.isEmpty())send(socket,Protocol::request("device.order.settled",{{"orderId",pending.orderId}},QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces)));
139:     }
140:     if(pending.client)send(pending.client,Protocol::response(pending.clientMessage,error.isEmpty()?0:400,error.isEmpty()?"ok":error,result));
141: }

I've thoroughly read the simulator code. Here's my understanding of the current communication flow and what could be improved:

## Current Simulator Communication Flow

1. **TLS Connection**: `Simulator::start()` uses `SecureConnect::connectToServer()` to establish TLS 1.2+ with the central server (`simulator.cpp:23-27`)
2. **Registration**: On TLS connect, sends `device.register` with token + charger codes (`simulator.cpp:29-35`)
3. **Heartbeat**: Every 5s, sends `device.heartbeat` per charger code (`simulator.cpp:78-82`)
4. **Telemetry**: Every 2s, if CHARGING, simulates voltage/current/power/SOC and sends `device.telemetry` (`simulator.cpp:84-99`)
5. **Sync**: After registration and when orders complete, sends `device.sync` with pending orders (`simulator.cpp:101-105`)
6. **Reconnect**: On disconnect, waits 3s and retries (`simulator.cpp:37-42`)

## Current Issues I Found

- **No explicit communication health check**: The simulator logs warnings on TLS errors (`simulator.cpp:26`) and disconnect (`simulator.cpp:40`), but there's no structured diagnostic output or health status reporting
- **No timeout on initial connection**: If the server isn't running, `SecureConnect::connectToServer` fails silently (logs warning, returns)
- **Reconnect has no backoff**: Fixed 3-second retry with no exponential backoff
- **No graceful shutdown notification**: When the simulator terminates, the server sees a disconnect but there's no "device.offline" message

36:     };
37:     for (const auto &migration : migrations)
38:         if (!ensureColumn(migration.table,migration.column,migration.definition,error)) return false;
39:     if (!executeScript(":/db/seed.sql", error)) return false;
40:     return true;
41: }
42: 
43: bool Database::ensureColumn(const QString &table,const QString &column,const QString &definition,QString *error)
44: {
45:     QSqlQuery info(m_db);
46:     if(!info.exec(QStringLiteral("PRAGMA table_info(%1)").arg(table))){if(error)*error=info.lastError().text();return false;}
47:     while(info.next())if(info.value(1).toString()==column)return true;
48:     QSqlQuery alter(m_db);
49:     if(!alter.exec(QStringLiteral("ALTER TABLE %1 ADD COLUMN %2 %3").arg(table,column,definition))){if(error)*error=alter.lastError().text();return false;}
50:     return true;
51: }
52: 
53: bool Database::executeScript(const QString &resourcePath, QString *error)
54: {
55:     QFile file(resourcePath);
56:     if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly | QIODevice::Text)) {
57:         if (error) *error = file.errorString();
58:         return false;
59:     }
60:     const QStringList statements = QString::fromUtf8(file.readAll()).split(';', Qt::SkipEmptyParts);
61:     for (const QString &raw : statements) {
62:         const QString sql = raw.trimmed();
63:         if (sql.isEmpty()) continue;
64:         QSqlQuery query(m_db);
65:         if (!query.exec(sql)) { if (error) *error = query.lastError().text() + " | " + sql.left(120); return false; }
66:     }
67:     return true;
68: }
69: 
70: bool Database::begin(QString *error)
71: {
72:     if (m_db.transaction()) return true;
73:     if (error) *error = m_db.lastError().text();
74:     return false;
75: }
76: bool Database::commit(QString *error)
77: {
78:     if (m_db.commit()) return true;
79:     if (error) *error = m_db.lastError().text();
80:     return false;
81: }
82: void Database::rollback() { m_db.rollback(); }
83: 
84: QJsonObject Database::registerUser(const QString &phone,const QString &password,QString *error)
85: {
86:     if(!PasswordUtils::validPhone(phone)){if(error)*error="手机号格式错误";return{};}
87:     if(!PasswordUtils::validPassword(password)){if(error)*error="密码长度须为 6～64 位";return{};}
88:     QSqlQuery existing(m_db);existing.prepare("SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE phone=?");existing.addBindValue(phone);
89:     if(!existing.exec()){if(error)*error=existing.lastError().text();return{};}
90:     const QString salt=PasswordUtils::createSalt(),hash=PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,salt);
91:     if(existing.next()){
92:         if(!existing.value(0).toString().isEmpty()){if(error)*error="该手机号已注册";return{};}
93:         QSqlQuery upgrade(m_db);upgrade.prepare("UPDATE users SET password_salt=?,password_hash=?,failed_attempts=0,status='NORMAL',locked_at=NULL WHERE phone=?");upgrade.addBindValue(salt);upgrade.addBindValue(hash);upgrade.addBindValue(phone);
94:         if(!upgrade.exec()){if(error)*error=upgrade.lastError().text();return{};}
95:     }else{
96:         QSqlQuery insert(m_db);insert.prepare("INSERT INTO users(phone,nickname,password_salt,password_hash,balance,status,failed_attempts,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,0,'NORMAL',0,?)");insert.addBindValue(phone);insert.addBindValue("用户"+phone.right(4));insert.addBindValue(salt);insert.addBindValue(hash);insert.addBindValue(now());
97:         if(!insert.exec()){if(error)*error=insert.lastError().text();return{};}
98:     }
99:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("SELECT id,phone,nickname,balance,status FROM users WHERE phone=?");q.addBindValue(phone);q.exec();q.next();
100:     return{{"id",q.value(0).toLongLong()},{"phone",q.value(1).toString()},{"nickname",q.value(2).toString()},{"balance",q.value(3).toDouble()},{"status",q.value(4).toString()}};
101: }
102: 
103: QJsonObject Database::loginUser(const QString &phone,const QString &password,QString *error)
104: {
105:     if(!PasswordUtils::validPhone(phone)||password.isEmpty()){if(error)*error="手机号或密码格式错误";return{};}
106:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);
107:     q.prepare("SELECT id,phone,nickname,balance,status,password_salt,password_hash,failed_attempts FROM users WHERE phone=?");
108:     q.addBindValue(phone);
109:     if (!q.exec()) { if (error) *error=q.lastError().text(); return {}; }
110:     if(!q.next()||q.value(6).toString().isEmpty()){if(error)*error="账号不存在，请先注册";return{};}
111:     if(q.value(4).toString()=="LOCKED"){if(error)*error="账户已锁定，请联系管理员解锁";return{};}
112:     if(q.value(4).toString()!="NORMAL"){if(error)*error="账户不可用，请联系管理员";return{};}
113:     const qint64 id=q.value(0).toLongLong();
114:     if(!PasswordUtils::constantTimeEquals(PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,q.value(5).toString()),q.value(6).toString())){
115:         const int attempts=q.value(7).toInt()+1;QSqlQuery fail(m_db);fail.prepare("UPDATE users SET failed_attempts=?,status=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN 'LOCKED' ELSE status END,locked_at=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN ? ELSE locked_at END WHERE id=?");fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(now());fail.addBindValue(id);fail.exec();
116:         if(error)*error=attempts>=5?"密码错误 5 次，账户已锁定":"密码错误，还可尝试 "+QString::number(5-attempts)+" 次";return{};
117:     }
118:     QSqlQuery reset(m_db);reset.prepare("UPDATE users SET failed_attempts=0,locked_at=NULL WHERE id=?");reset.addBindValue(id);reset.exec();
119:     return {{"id", q.value(0).toLongLong()}, {"phone", q.value(1).toString()},
120:             {"nickname", q.value(2).toString()}, {"balance", q.value(3).toDouble()},
121:             {"status", q.value(4).toString()}};
122: }

124: QJsonObject Database::recharge(qint64 userId,double amount,const QString &password,QString *error)
125: {
126:     if (amount <= 0 || amount > 10000) {
127:         if (error) *error = "充值金额必须在 0 到 10000 元之间";
128:         return {};
129:     }
130:     QSqlQuery auth(m_db);auth.prepare("SELECT password_salt,password_hash,status,failed_attempts FROM users WHERE id=?");auth.addBindValue(userId);
131:     if(!auth.exec()||!auth.next()){if(error)*error="请先登录";return{};}
132:     if(auth.value(2).toString()=="LOCKED"){if(error)*error="账户已锁定，请联系管理员解锁";return{};}
133:     if(auth.value(2).toString()!="NORMAL"){if(error)*error="账户不可用";return{};}
134:     if(!PasswordUtils::constantTimeEquals(PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,auth.value(0).toString()),auth.value(1).toString())){
135:         const int attempts=auth.value(3).toInt()+1;QSqlQuery fail(m_db);fail.prepare("UPDATE users SET failed_attempts=?,status=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN 'LOCKED' ELSE status END,locked_at=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN ? ELSE locked_at END WHERE id=?");fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(now());fail.addBindValue(userId);fail.exec();
136:         if(error)*error=attempts>=5?"密码错误 5 次，账户已锁定":"充值密码错误，还可尝试 "+QString::number(5-attempts)+" 次";return{};
137:     }
138:     QSqlQuery reset(m_db);reset.prepare("UPDATE users SET failed_attempts=0,locked_at=NULL WHERE id=?");reset.addBindValue(userId);reset.exec();
139:     if (!begin(error)) return {};
140:     QSqlQuery update(m_db);
141:     update.prepare("UPDATE users SET balance=balance+? WHERE id=? AND status='NORMAL'");
142:     update.addBindValue(amount); update.addBindValue(userId);
143:     if (!update.exec() || update.numRowsAffected()!=1) {
144:         rollback(); if(error)*error="用户不存在或已冻结"; return {};
145:     }
146:     QSqlQuery balance(m_db);
147:     balance.prepare("SELECT balance FROM users WHERE id=?"); balance.addBindValue(userId);
148:     if(!balance.exec()||!balance.next()){rollback();if(error)*error=balance.lastError().text();return{};}
149:     const double value=balance.value(0).toDouble();
150:     QSqlQuery flow(m_db);
151:     flow.prepare("INSERT INTO wallet_transactions(user_id,type,amount,balance_after,created_at) VALUES(?,'RECHARGE',?,?,?)");
152:     flow.addBindValue(userId);flow.addBindValue(amount);flow.addBindValue(value);flow.addBindValue(now());
153:     if(!flow.exec()||!commit(error)){rollback();return{};}
154:     return{{"balance",value}};
155: }

157: QJsonArray Database::userOrders(qint64 userId,QString *error)
158: {
159:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("SELECT o.id,s.name,c.code,o.status,o.mode,o.energy,o.duration,o.amount,o.start_at,COALESCE(o.end_at,'--') FROM charge_orders o JOIN chargers c ON c.id=o.charger_id JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id WHERE o.user_id=? ORDER BY o.id DESC LIMIT 50");q.addBindValue(userId);
160:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return{};}QJsonArray a;while(q.next())a.append(QJsonObject{{"id",q.value(0).toLongLong()},{"station",q.value(1).toString()},{"charger",q.value(2).toString()},{"status",q.value(3).toString()},{"mode",q.value(4).toString()},{"energy",q.value(5).toDouble()},{"duration",q.value(6).toInt()},{"amount",q.value(7).toDouble()},{"startAt",q.value(8).toString()},{"endAt",q.value(9).toString()}});return a;
161: }

163: bool Database::loginAdmin(const QString &username, const QString &password, QString *error)
164: {
165:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);
166:     q.prepare("SELECT id,password_hash,status,COALESCE(password_salt,''),failed_attempts FROM admins WHERE username=?"); q.addBindValue(username);
167:     if (!q.exec()) { if (error) *error=q.lastError().text(); return false; }
168:     if (!q.next()) { if (error) *error="账号或密码错误"; return false; }
169:     if(q.value(2).toString()=="LOCKED"){if(error)*error="管理员账户已锁定，需要在数据库中由系统负责人解锁";return false;}
170:     if(q.value(2).toString()!="NORMAL"){if(error)*error="管理员账户不可用";return false;}
171:     const qint64 id=q.value(0).toLongLong();const QString stored=q.value(1).toString(),salt=q.value(3).toString();
172:     bool matched=false;
173:     if(salt.isEmpty())matched=PasswordUtils::constantTimeEquals(legacyHash(password),stored);
174:     else matched=PasswordUtils::constantTimeEquals(PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,salt),stored);
175:     if(!matched){
176:         const int attempts=q.value(4).toInt()+1;QSqlQuery fail(m_db);fail.prepare("UPDATE admins SET failed_attempts=?,status=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN 'LOCKED' ELSE status END,locked_at=CASE WHEN ?>=5 THEN ? ELSE locked_at END WHERE id=?");fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(attempts);fail.addBindValue(now());fail.addBindValue(id);fail.exec();
177:         if(error)*error=attempts>=5?"密码错误 5 次，管理员账户已锁定":"账号或密码错误，还可尝试 "+QString::number(5-attempts)+" 次";return false;
178:     }
179:     QSqlQuery reset(m_db);
180:     if(salt.isEmpty()){const QString newSalt=PasswordUtils::createSalt();reset.prepare("UPDATE admins SET password_salt=?,password_hash=?,failed_attempts=0,locked_at=NULL WHERE id=?");reset.addBindValue(newSalt);reset.addBindValue(PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,newSalt));reset.addBindValue(id);}
181:     else{reset.prepare("UPDATE admins SET failed_attempts=0,locked_at=NULL WHERE id=?");reset.addBindValue(id);}reset.exec();
182:     return true;
183: }
184: 
185: bool Database::registerAdmin(const QString &username,const QString &password,QString *error)
186: {
187:     if(!QRegularExpression(QStringLiteral("^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,31}$")).match(username).hasMatch()){if(error)*error="管理员账号须为 4～32 位字母、数字或下划线，并以字母开头";return false;}
188:     if(!PasswordUtils::validPassword(password)){if(error)*error="管理员密码长度须为 6～64 位";return false;}
189:     const QString salt=PasswordUtils::createSalt();QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("INSERT INTO admins(username,password_salt,password_hash,role,status,failed_attempts) VALUES(?,?,?,'ADMIN','NORMAL',0)");q.addBindValue(username);q.addBindValue(salt);q.addBindValue(PasswordUtils::hashPassword(password,salt));
190:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().nativeErrorCode()=="19"?"管理员账号已存在":q.lastError().text();return false;}
191:     QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','ADD_ADMIN','ADMIN',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(q.lastInsertId());log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return true;
192: }

194: QJsonArray Database::stationList(QString *error)
195: {
196:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);
197:     const char *sql = "SELECT s.id,s.name,s.address,s.longitude,s.latitude,s.base_price,"
198:                       "COUNT(c.id),SUM(CASE WHEN c.status='IDLE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),"
199:                       "MIN(CASE WHEN c.status='IDLE' THEN c.id END) "
200:                       "FROM stations s LEFT JOIN chargers c ON c.station_id=s.id "
201:                       "GROUP BY s.id ORDER BY s.id";
202:     if (!q.exec(sql)) { if (error) *error=q.lastError().text(); return {}; }
203:     QJsonArray result;
204:     while(q.next()) result.append(QJsonObject{{"id",q.value(0).toLongLong()},{"name",q.value(1).toString()},
205:         {"address",q.value(2).toString()},{"longitude",q.value(3).toDouble()},
206:         {"latitude",q.value(4).toDouble()},{"price",q.value(5).toDouble()},
207:         {"total",q.value(6).toInt()},{"idle",q.value(7).toInt()},
208:         {"chargerId",q.value(8).toLongLong()}});
209:     return result;
210: }

212: QJsonObject Database::createReservation(qint64 userId, qint64 chargerId, QString *error)
213: {
214:     if (!begin(error)) return {};
215:     QSqlQuery check(m_db);
216:     check.prepare("SELECT status FROM users WHERE id=?"); check.addBindValue(userId);
217:     if (!check.exec() || !check.next() || check.value(0).toString()!="NORMAL") { rollback(); if(error)*error="用户不可预约"; return {}; }
218:     QSqlQuery busy(m_db);
219:     busy.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM charge_orders WHERE user_id=? AND status IN ('CHARGING','PENDING_PAYMENT')"); busy.addBindValue(userId);
220:     if (!busy.exec() || busy.next()) { rollback(); if(error)*error="存在未完成订单"; return {}; }
221:     QSqlQuery lock(m_db);
222:     lock.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='RESERVED' WHERE id=? AND status='IDLE'"); lock.addBindValue(chargerId);
223:     if (!lock.exec() || lock.numRowsAffected()!=1) { rollback(); if(error)*error="充电桩当前不可预约"; return {}; }
224:     const QDateTime expires=QDateTime::currentDateTimeUtc().addSecs(20*60);
225:     QSqlQuery insert(m_db);
226:     insert.prepare("INSERT INTO reservations(user_id,charger_id,status,expires_at,created_at) VALUES(?,?,'ACTIVE',?,?)");
227:     insert.addBindValue(userId); insert.addBindValue(chargerId); insert.addBindValue(expires.toString(Qt::ISODate)); insert.addBindValue(now());
228:     if (!insert.exec() || !commit(error)) { rollback(); if(error && error->isEmpty())*error=insert.lastError().text(); return {}; }
229:     return {{"reservationId",insert.lastInsertId().toLongLong()},{"expiresAt",expires.toString(Qt::ISODate)}};
230: }
231: 
232: bool Database::cancelReservation(qint64 userId, qint64 reservationId, const QString &reason, QString *error)
233: {
234:     if (!begin(error)) return false;
235:     QSqlQuery find(m_db); find.prepare("SELECT charger_id FROM reservations WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='ACTIVE'");
236:     find.addBindValue(reservationId); find.addBindValue(userId);
237:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="预约不存在或已结束";return false;}
238:     const qint64 chargerId=find.value(0).toLongLong();
239:     QSqlQuery q(m_db); q.prepare("UPDATE reservations SET status='CANCELLED',cancel_reason=? WHERE id=?"); q.addBindValue(reason);q.addBindValue(reservationId);
240:     if(!q.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return false;}
241:     QSqlQuery release(m_db);release.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='IDLE' WHERE id=? AND status='RESERVED'");release.addBindValue(chargerId);
242:     if(!release.exec()||!commit(error)){rollback();return false;} return true;
243: }

245: QJsonObject Database::startCharge(qint64 userId, qint64 chargerId, const QString &mode, double target, QString *error)
246: {
247:     const QJsonObject pending=createPendingCharge(userId,chargerId,mode,target,error);
248:     if(pending.isEmpty())return{};
249:     return activatePendingCharge(userId,pending.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong(),error);
250: }
251: 
252: QJsonObject Database::createPendingCharge(qint64 userId,qint64 chargerId,const QString &mode,double target,QString *error)
253: {
254:     if(target<=0||!QStringList({"AMOUNT","ENERGY","TIME"}).contains(mode)){if(error)*error="充电目标无效";return{};}
255:     if(!begin(error))return{};
256:     QSqlQuery quote(m_db);
257:     quote.prepare("SELECT u.balance,u.status,c.code,c.rated_power,c.status,s.base_price "
258:                   "FROM users u,chargers c JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id "
259:                   "WHERE u.id=? AND c.id=?");
260:     quote.addBindValue(userId);quote.addBindValue(chargerId);
261:     if(!quote.exec()||!quote.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="用户或充电桩不存在";return{};}
262:     const double balance=quote.value(0).toDouble();
263:     const QString userStatus=quote.value(1).toString();
264:     const QString chargerCode=quote.value(2).toString();
265:     const double ratedPower=quote.value(3).toDouble();
266:     const QString chargerStatus=quote.value(4).toString();
267:     const double price=quote.value(5).toDouble();
268:     if(userStatus!="NORMAL"){rollback();if(error)*error="用户状态异常";return{};}
269:     if(!QStringList({"IDLE","RESERVED"}).contains(chargerStatus)){rollback();if(error)*error="充电桩不可用";return{};}
270:     double required=target;
271:     if(mode=="ENERGY")required=target*price;
272:     else if(mode=="TIME")required=(target/60.0)*ratedPower*price;
273:     required=qRound64(required*100.0)/100.0;
274:     if(balance+0.0001<required){rollback();if(error)*error=QString("余额不足，预计至少需要 ¥%1（当前 ¥%2）").arg(required,0,'f',2).arg(balance,0,'f',2);return{};}
275:     QSqlQuery busy(m_db);busy.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM charge_orders WHERE user_id=? AND status IN ('STARTING','CHARGING','PENDING_PAYMENT')");busy.addBindValue(userId);
276:     if(!busy.exec()||busy.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="存在未完成订单";return{};}
277:     QSqlQuery lock(m_db);lock.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='STARTING' WHERE id=? AND status IN ('IDLE','RESERVED')");lock.addBindValue(chargerId);
278:     if(!lock.exec()||lock.numRowsAffected()!=1){rollback();if(error)*error="充电桩不可用";return{};}
279:     QSqlQuery insert(m_db);insert.prepare("INSERT INTO charge_orders(user_id,charger_id,status,mode,target,start_at,energy,duration,amount) VALUES(?,?,'STARTING',?,?,?,0,0,0)");
280:     insert.addBindValue(userId);insert.addBindValue(chargerId);insert.addBindValue(mode);insert.addBindValue(target);insert.addBindValue(now());
281:     if(!insert.exec()||!commit(error)){rollback();if(error&&error->isEmpty())*error=insert.lastError().text();return{};}
282:     return{{"orderId",insert.lastInsertId().toLongLong()},{"chargerId",chargerId},{"chargerCode",chargerCode},{"mode",mode},{"target",target},{"basePrice",price},{"ratedPower",ratedPower},{"requiredAmount",required}};
283: }
284: 
285: QJsonObject Database::activatePendingCharge(qint64 userId,qint64 orderId,QString *error)
286: {
287:     if(!begin(error))return{};
288:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);find.prepare("SELECT charger_id FROM charge_orders WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='STARTING'");find.addBindValue(orderId);find.addBindValue(userId);
289:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="待启动订单不存在";return{};}
290:     const qint64 chargerId=find.value(0).toLongLong();
291:     QSqlQuery order(m_db);order.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET status='CHARGING' WHERE id=?");order.addBindValue(orderId);
292:     QSqlQuery charger(m_db);charger.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='CHARGING' WHERE id=? AND status IN ('STARTING','IDLE','RESERVED','OFFLINE')");charger.addBindValue(chargerId);
293:     if(!order.exec()||!charger.exec()||charger.numRowsAffected()!=1){rollback();if(error)*error="订单激活失败";return{};}
294:     QSqlQuery reservation(m_db);reservation.prepare("UPDATE reservations SET status='USED' WHERE user_id=? AND charger_id=? AND status='ACTIVE'");reservation.addBindValue(userId);reservation.addBindValue(chargerId);reservation.exec();
295:     if(!commit(error)){rollback();return{};}
296:     return{{"orderId",orderId},{"status","CHARGING"}};
297: }
298: 
299: bool Database::cancelPendingCharge(qint64 orderId,const QString &reason,QString *error)
300: {
301:     if(!begin(error))return false;
302:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);find.prepare("SELECT charger_id,user_id FROM charge_orders WHERE id=? AND status='STARTING'");find.addBindValue(orderId);
303:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){rollback();return true;}
304:     const qint64 chargerId=find.value(0).toLongLong(),userId=find.value(1).toLongLong();
305:     QSqlQuery order(m_db);order.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET status='CANCELLED',end_at=? WHERE id=?");order.addBindValue(now());order.addBindValue(orderId);
306:     QSqlQuery charger(m_db);charger.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status=CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE user_id=? AND charger_id=? AND status='ACTIVE') THEN 'RESERVED' ELSE 'IDLE' END WHERE id=? AND status='STARTING'");charger.addBindValue(userId);charger.addBindValue(chargerId);charger.addBindValue(chargerId);
307:     if(!order.exec()||!charger.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=order.lastError().text();return false;}
308:     QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('SYSTEM','CANCEL_DEVICE_START','ORDER',?,?,?)");log.addBindValue(orderId);log.addBindValue(reason);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();
309:     if(!commit(error)){rollback();return false;}return true;
310: }

312: QJsonObject Database::activeOrderForStop(qint64 userId,qint64 orderId,QString *error)
313: {
314:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("SELECT o.id,o.charger_id,c.code FROM charge_orders o JOIN chargers c ON c.id=o.charger_id WHERE o.id=? AND o.user_id=? AND o.status='CHARGING'");q.addBindValue(orderId);q.addBindValue(userId);
315:     if(!q.exec()||!q.next()){if(error)*error="活动订单不存在";return{};}
316:     return{{"orderId",q.value(0).toLongLong()},{"chargerId",q.value(1).toLongLong()},{"chargerCode",q.value(2).toString()}};
317: }
318: 
319: QJsonObject Database::completeChargeFromDevice(qint64 orderId,double energy,int duration,const QString &endAt,QString *error)
320: {
321:     if(energy<0||duration<0){if(error)*error="充电桩上报的结算数据无效";return{};}
322:     if(!begin(error))return{};
323:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);find.prepare("SELECT o.user_id,o.charger_id,o.status,s.base_price FROM charge_orders o JOIN chargers c ON c.id=o.charger_id JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id WHERE o.id=?");find.addBindValue(orderId);
324:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="中心订单不存在";return{};}
325:     const qint64 userId=find.value(0).toLongLong(),chargerId=find.value(1).toLongLong();
326:     const QString oldStatus=find.value(2).toString();
327:     if(oldStatus=="COMPLETED"){QSqlQuery done(m_db);done.prepare("SELECT amount,energy,duration FROM charge_orders WHERE id=?");done.addBindValue(orderId);done.exec();done.next();rollback();return{{"orderId",orderId},{"userId",userId},{"amount",done.value(0).toDouble()},{"energy",done.value(1).toDouble()},{"duration",done.value(2).toInt()},{"status","COMPLETED"}};}
328:     if(oldStatus!="CHARGING"&&oldStatus!="STARTING"){rollback();if(error)*error="订单状态不可结算";return{};}
329:     const double amount=qRound64(energy*find.value(3).toDouble()*100.0)/100.0;
330:     QSqlQuery wallet(m_db);wallet.prepare("UPDATE users SET balance=balance-? WHERE id=? AND balance>=?");wallet.addBindValue(amount);wallet.addBindValue(userId);wallet.addBindValue(amount);
331:     if(!wallet.exec()||wallet.numRowsAffected()!=1){rollback();if(error)*error="余额不足，订单需人工处理";return{};}
332:     QSqlQuery update(m_db);update.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET status='COMPLETED',end_at=?,energy=?,duration=?,amount=? WHERE id=?");update.addBindValue(endAt.isEmpty()?now():endAt);update.addBindValue(energy);update.addBindValue(duration);update.addBindValue(amount);update.addBindValue(orderId);
333:     if(!update.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=update.lastError().text();return{};}
334:     QSqlQuery flow(m_db);flow.prepare("INSERT INTO wallet_transactions(user_id,type,amount,balance_after,related_order_id,created_at) SELECT id,'CHARGE',-?,balance,?,? FROM users WHERE id=?");flow.addBindValue(amount);flow.addBindValue(orderId);flow.addBindValue(now());flow.addBindValue(userId);
335:     QSqlQuery release(m_db);release.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='IDLE',total_sessions=total_sessions+1,total_duration=total_duration+? WHERE id=?");release.addBindValue(duration);release.addBindValue(chargerId);
336:     if(!flow.exec()||!release.exec()||!commit(error)){rollback();return{};}
337:     return{{"orderId",orderId},{"userId",userId},{"amount",amount},{"energy",energy},{"duration",duration},{"status","COMPLETED"}};
338: }

340: QJsonObject Database::syncDeviceOrder(qint64 orderId,const QString &chargerCode,const QString &status,double energy,int duration,const QString &endAt,QString *error)
341: {
342:     QSqlQuery owner(m_db);owner.prepare("SELECT o.id FROM charge_orders o JOIN chargers c ON c.id=o.charger_id WHERE o.id=? AND c.code=?");owner.addBindValue(orderId);owner.addBindValue(chargerCode);
343:     if(!owner.exec()||!owner.next()){if(error)*error="订单与充电桩不匹配";return{};}
344:     if(status=="SYNC_PENDING"||status=="COMPLETED")return completeChargeFromDevice(orderId,energy,duration,endAt,error);
345:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET status='CHARGING',energy=MAX(energy,?),duration=MAX(duration,?) WHERE id=? AND status IN ('STARTING','CHARGING')");q.addBindValue(energy);q.addBindValue(duration);q.addBindValue(orderId);
346:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return{};}
347:     updateHeartbeat(chargerCode,"CHARGING",error);
348:     return{{"orderId",orderId},{"status","CHARGING"}};
349: }

351: QJsonObject Database::stopCharge(qint64 userId, qint64 orderId, QString *error)
352: {
353:     if(!begin(error))return{};
354:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);find.prepare("SELECT charger_id,energy,start_at FROM charge_orders WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='CHARGING'");find.addBindValue(orderId);find.addBindValue(userId);
355:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){rollback();if(error)*error="活动订单不存在";return{};}
356:     const qint64 chargerId=find.value(0).toLongLong(); const double energy=find.value(1).toDouble();
357:     const int elapsed=qMax(1,static_cast<int>(QDateTime::fromString(find.value(2).toString(),Qt::ISODate).secsTo(QDateTime::currentDateTimeUtc())));
358:     QSqlQuery price(m_db);price.prepare("SELECT s.base_price FROM chargers c JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id WHERE c.id=?");price.addBindValue(chargerId);price.exec();price.next();
359:     const double amount=qRound64(energy*price.value(0).toDouble()*100.0)/100.0;
360:     QSqlQuery update(m_db);update.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET status='COMPLETED',end_at=?,amount=?,duration=MAX(duration,?) WHERE id=?");update.addBindValue(now());update.addBindValue(amount);update.addBindValue(elapsed);update.addBindValue(orderId);
361:     if(!update.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=update.lastError().text();return{};}
362:     QSqlQuery wallet(m_db);wallet.prepare("UPDATE users SET balance=balance-? WHERE id=? AND balance>=?");wallet.addBindValue(amount);wallet.addBindValue(userId);wallet.addBindValue(amount);
363:     if(!wallet.exec()||wallet.numRowsAffected()!=1){rollback();if(error)*error="余额不足，订单转人工结算";return{};}
364:     QSqlQuery flow(m_db);flow.prepare("INSERT INTO wallet_transactions(user_id,type,amount,balance_after,related_order_id,created_at) SELECT id,'CHARGE',-?,balance,?,? FROM users WHERE id=?");flow.addBindValue(amount);flow.addBindValue(orderId);flow.addBindValue(now());flow.addBindValue(userId);if(!flow.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=flow.lastError().text();return{};}
365:     QSqlQuery release(m_db);release.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='IDLE',total_sessions=total_sessions+1,total_duration=total_duration+? WHERE id=?");release.addBindValue(elapsed);release.addBindValue(chargerId);release.exec();
366:     if(!commit(error)){rollback();return{};} return {{"orderId",orderId},{"amount",amount},{"energy",energy},{"status","COMPLETED"}};
367: }

369: bool Database::updateHeartbeat(const QString &chargerCode, const QString &status, QString *error)
370: {
371:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);
372:     q.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status=?,last_seen=? WHERE code=?");
373:     q.addBindValue(status);q.addBindValue(now());q.addBindValue(chargerCode);
374:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return false;}return q.numRowsAffected()==1;
375: }

377: bool Database::markDeviceOffline(const QStringList &chargerCodes,QString *error)
378: {

380:     for(const QString &code:chargerCodes){q.bindValue(0,code);if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return false;}}
381:     return true;

384: bool Database::insertTelemetry(const QString &chargerCode,double voltage,double current,double power,double soc,QString *error)
385: {
386:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);find.prepare("SELECT id FROM chargers WHERE code=?");find.addBindValue(chargerCode);
387:     if(!find.exec()||!find.next()){if(error)*error="未知设备";return false;} const qint64 chargerId=find.value(0).toLongLong();
388:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("INSERT INTO telemetry(charger_id,sampled_at,voltage,current,power,soc) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)");
389:     q.addBindValue(chargerId);q.addBindValue(now());q.addBindValue(voltage);q.addBindValue(current);q.addBindValue(power);q.addBindValue(soc);
390:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return false;}
391:     QSqlQuery order(m_db);
392:     order.prepare("UPDATE charge_orders SET energy=energy+(?/1800.0),duration=duration+2 WHERE charger_id=? AND status='CHARGING'");
393:     order.addBindValue(power);order.addBindValue(chargerId);
394:     if(!order.exec()){if(error)*error=order.lastError().text();return false;}
395:     return true;
396: }

398: QJsonObject Database::adminSummary(QString *error)

402:     if(!q.exec("SELECT COUNT(*),SUM(CASE WHEN status='IDLE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),SUM(CASE WHEN status='CHARGING' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),SUM(CASE WHEN status='FAULT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) FROM chargers")||!q.next()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return{};}
403:     result["chargers"]=q.value(0).toInt();result["idle"]=q.value(1).toInt();result["charging"]=q.value(2).toInt();result["fault"]=q.value(3).toInt();
404:     QSqlQuery metrics(m_db);
405:     if(!metrics.exec("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN date(end_at)=date('now','localtime') THEN amount END),0),"
406:                      "COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN strftime('%Y-%m',end_at)=strftime('%Y-%m','now','localtime') THEN amount END),0),"
407:                      "COALESCE(SUM(amount),0),SUM(CASE WHEN date(start_at)=date('now','localtime') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) "
408:                      "FROM charge_orders WHERE status='COMPLETED'")||!metrics.next()){if(error)*error=metrics.lastError().text();return{};}
409:     result["todayRevenue"]=metrics.value(0).toDouble();result["monthRevenue"]=metrics.value(1).toDouble();
410:     result["revenue"]=metrics.value(2).toDouble();result["todayOrders"]=metrics.value(3).toInt();
411:     QSqlQuery users(m_db);if(users.exec("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users")&&users.next())result["users"]=users.value(0).toInt();
412:     QJsonArray trend;
413:     QSqlQuery tq(m_db);tq.exec("WITH RECURSIVE days(d) AS (SELECT date('now','localtime','-6 day') UNION ALL SELECT date(d,'+1 day') FROM days WHERE d<date('now','localtime')) SELECT d,COALESCE(SUM(o.amount),0) FROM days LEFT JOIN charge_orders o ON date(o.end_at)=d AND o.status='COMPLETED' GROUP BY d ORDER BY d");
414:     while(tq.next())trend.append(QJsonObject{{"date",tq.value(0).toString()},{"amount",tq.value(1).toDouble()}});result["revenueTrend"]=trend;
415:     QJsonArray ranking;
416:     QSqlQuery rq(m_db);rq.exec("SELECT s.name,COALESCE(SUM(o.amount),0) revenue FROM stations s LEFT JOIN chargers c ON c.station_id=s.id LEFT JOIN charge_orders o ON o.charger_id=c.id AND o.status='COMPLETED' GROUP BY s.id ORDER BY revenue DESC LIMIT 5");
417:     while(rq.next())ranking.append(QJsonObject{{"name",rq.value(0).toString()},{"revenue",rq.value(1).toDouble()}});result["stationRanking"]=ranking;
418:     QJsonArray active;
419:     QSqlQuery aq(m_db);aq.exec("SELECT o.id,u.phone,s.name,c.code,o.energy,o.duration FROM charge_orders o JOIN users u ON u.id=o.user_id JOIN chargers c ON c.id=o.charger_id JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id WHERE o.status='CHARGING' ORDER BY o.id DESC LIMIT 8");
420:     while(aq.next())active.append(QJsonObject{{"id",aq.value(0).toLongLong()},{"phone",aq.value(1).toString()},{"station",aq.value(2).toString()},{"charger",aq.value(3).toString()},{"energy",aq.value(4).toDouble()},{"duration",aq.value(5).toInt()}});result["activeOrders"]=active;
421:     QJsonArray faults;
422:     QSqlQuery fq(m_db);fq.exec("SELECT c.id,c.code,s.name,COALESCE(c.last_seen,'--') FROM chargers c JOIN stations s ON s.id=c.station_id WHERE c.status='FAULT' ORDER BY c.id DESC LIMIT 8");
423:     while(fq.next())faults.append(QJsonObject{{"id",fq.value(0).toLongLong()},{"code",fq.value(1).toString()},{"station",fq.value(2).toString()},{"lastSeen",fq.value(3).toString()}});result["faultChargers"]=faults;
424:     return result;
425: }

457: QJsonObject Database::addStation(const QJsonObject &s,QString *error)
458: {
459:     const QString name=s.value("name").toString().trimmed(),address=s.value("address").toString().trimmed();const double lon=s.value("longitude").toDouble(),lat=s.value("latitude").toDouble(),price=s.value("price").toDouble();
460:     if(name.isEmpty()||address.isEmpty()||lon<-180||lon>180||lat<-90||lat>90||price<=0){if(error)*error="请填写有效的站名、地址、经纬度和基础电价";return{};}
461:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("INSERT INTO stations(name,address,longitude,latitude,base_price,status) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'ONLINE')");q.addBindValue(name);q.addBindValue(address);q.addBindValue(lon);q.addBindValue(lat);q.addBindValue(price);if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().text();return{};}const qint64 id=q.lastInsertId().toLongLong();QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','ADD_STATION','STATION',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(id);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return{{"id",id}};
462: }

464: bool Database::updateStation(const QJsonObject &s,QString *error)
465: {
466:     const qint64 id=s.value("stationId").toVariant().toLongLong();
467:     const QString name=s.value("name").toString().trimmed(),address=s.value("address").toString().trimmed(),status=s.value("status").toString();
468:     const double lon=s.value("longitude").toDouble(),lat=s.value("latitude").toDouble(),price=s.value("price").toDouble();
469:     if(id<=0||name.isEmpty()||address.isEmpty()||lon<-180||lon>180||lat<-90||lat>90||price<=0||!QStringList({"ONLINE","OFFLINE","MAINTENANCE"}).contains(status)){if(error)*error="请填写有效的电站资料和状态";return false;}
470:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("UPDATE stations SET name=?,address=?,longitude=?,latitude=?,base_price=?,status=? WHERE id=?");
471:     q.addBindValue(name);q.addBindValue(address);q.addBindValue(lon);q.addBindValue(lat);q.addBindValue(price);q.addBindValue(status);q.addBindValue(id);
472:     if(!q.exec()||q.numRowsAffected()!=1){if(error)*error="电站不存在或保存失败："+q.lastError().text();return false;}
473:     QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','UPDATE_STATION','STATION',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(id);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return true;
474: }
