476: bool Database::deleteStation(qint64 id,QString *error)
477: {
478:     QSqlQuery used(m_db);used.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chargers WHERE station_id=?");used.addBindValue(id);
479:     if(!used.exec()||!used.next()){if(error)*error=used.lastError().text();return false;}
480:     if(used.value(0).toInt()>0){if(error)*error="该电站仍有关联电桩，请先处理电桩后再删除";return false;}
481:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("DELETE FROM stations WHERE id=?");q.addBindValue(id);
482:     if(!q.exec()||q.numRowsAffected()!=1){if(error)*error="电站不存在或删除失败："+q.lastError().text();return false;}
483:     QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','DELETE_STATION','STATION',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(id);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return true;
484: }

486: QJsonObject Database::addCharger(const QJsonObject &c,QString *error)
487: {
488:     const qint64 stationId=c.value("stationId").toVariant().toLongLong();
489:     const QString code=c.value("code").toString().trimmed().toUpper(),type=c.value("chargerType").toString(),status=c.value("status").toString("IDLE");
490:     const double power=c.value("power").toDouble();
491:     if(stationId<=0||!QRegularExpression("^[A-Z0-9_-]{3,32}$").match(code).hasMatch()||!QStringList({"FAST","SLOW"}).contains(type)||power<=0||power>1000||!QStringList({"IDLE","OFFLINE","FAULT"}).contains(status)){if(error)*error="请填写有效的所属电站、设备编号、类型、功率和状态";return{};}
492:     QSqlQuery station(m_db);station.prepare("SELECT id FROM stations WHERE id=?");station.addBindValue(stationId);if(!station.exec()||!station.next()){if(error)*error="所属电站不存在";return{};}
493:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("INSERT INTO chargers(station_id,code,type,rated_power,status,total_sessions,total_duration) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,0,0)");q.addBindValue(stationId);q.addBindValue(code);q.addBindValue(type);q.addBindValue(power);q.addBindValue(status);
494:     if(!q.exec()){if(error)*error=q.lastError().nativeErrorCode()=="2067"?"设备编号已存在":q.lastError().text();return{};}
495:     const qint64 id=q.lastInsertId().toLongLong();QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','ADD_CHARGER','CHARGER',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(id);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return{{"id",id}};
496: }

498: bool Database::updateCharger(const QJsonObject &c,QString *error)
499: {
500:     const qint64 id=c.value("chargerId").toVariant().toLongLong(),stationId=c.value("stationId").toVariant().toLongLong();
501:     const QString code=c.value("code").toString().trimmed().toUpper(),type=c.value("chargerType").toString(),status=c.value("status").toString();const double power=c.value("power").toDouble();
502:     if(id<=0||stationId<=0||!QRegularExpression("^[A-Z0-9_-]{3,32}$").match(code).hasMatch()||!QStringList({"FAST","SLOW"}).contains(type)||power<=0||power>1000||!QStringList({"IDLE","OFFLINE","FAULT"}).contains(status)){if(error)*error="请填写有效的电桩资料";return false;}
503:     QSqlQuery current(m_db);current.prepare("SELECT status FROM chargers WHERE id=?");current.addBindValue(id);if(!current.exec()||!current.next()){if(error)*error="电桩不存在";return false;}if(QStringList({"CHARGING","RESERVED"}).contains(current.value(0).toString())){if(error)*error="正在充电或已预约的电桩不能修改";return false;}
504:     QSqlQuery q(m_db);q.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET station_id=?,code=?,type=?,rated_power=?,status=? WHERE id=?");q.addBindValue(stationId);q.addBindValue(code);q.addBindValue(type);q.addBindValue(power);q.addBindValue(status);q.addBindValue(id);
505:     if(!q.exec()||q.numRowsAffected()!=1){if(error)*error=q.lastError().nativeErrorCode()=="2067"?"设备编号已存在":q.lastError().text();return false;}
506:     QSqlQuery log(m_db);log.prepare("INSERT INTO operation_logs(actor_type,action,target_type,target_id,result,created_at) VALUES('ADMIN','UPDATE_CHARGER','CHARGER',?,'SUCCESS',?)");log.addBindValue(id);log.addBindValue(now());log.exec();return true;
507: }

509: bool Database::deleteCharger(qint64 id,QString *error)
510: {

527: int Database::expireReservations(QString *error)

530:     QSqlQuery find(m_db);
531:     find.prepare("SELECT id,charger_id FROM reservations WHERE status='ACTIVE' AND expires_at<=?");
532:     find.addBindValue(now());
533:     if(!find.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=find.lastError().text();return -1;}
534:     QList<qint64> ids,chargers;
535:     while(find.next()){ids.append(find.value(0).toLongLong());chargers.append(find.value(1).toLongLong());}
536:     QSqlQuery cancel(m_db),release(m_db);
537:     for(int i=0;i<ids.size();++i){
538:         cancel.prepare("UPDATE reservations SET status='CANCELLED',cancel_reason='TIMEOUT' WHERE id=? AND status='ACTIVE'");cancel.addBindValue(ids[i]);
539:         if(!cancel.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=cancel.lastError().text();return -1;}
540:         release.prepare("UPDATE chargers SET status='IDLE' WHERE id=? AND status='RESERVED'");release.addBindValue(chargers[i]);
541:         if(!release.exec()){rollback();if(error)*error=release.lastError().text();return -1;}
542:     }
543:     if(!commit(error)){rollback();return -1;}return ids.size();

## Assistant (Plan · MiMo V2.5 Free · 62.2s)

_Thinking:_

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the entire communication flow. Let me compile my analysis of communication issues between the simulator and server.

## Communication Issues Analysis

### 1. Token Mismatch (Most Common)
- Server checks `device/token` in `app.ini` vs `--token` CLI arg
- If mismatched, registration is rejected with "设备凭据错误" (serverapp.cpp:203)
- Simulator logs: "device registration rejected" and disconnects (simulator.cpp:58)

### 2. TLS Connection Failures
- `SecureConnect::connectToServer` checks `QSslSocket::supportsSsl()` first
- If OpenSSL is missing, the error is "当前环境缺少 TLS/OpenSSL 运行库"
- Server cert is embedded in clients via qrc (`:/tls/server-cert.pem`)
- Server uses its own cert/key from config for TLS handshake
- If cert/key files don't exist or are invalid, server fails to start

### 3. Server Not Running
- Simulator tries to connect, gets connection refused
- Logs warning: "当前环境缺少 TLS/OpenSSL 运行库" or connection error
- Reconnects after 3 seconds (simulator.cpp:41)
- No exponential backoff - keeps retrying every 3 seconds

### 4. Charger Code Mismatch
- Server validates charger codes during registration (serverapp.cpp:206-213)
- If the charger code doesn't exist in the central database, `updateHeartbeat` returns false
- The registration still proceeds but the charger might not be properly tracked### 5. No Explicit Health Check Protocol
- There's no "ping" or "health check" message type
- Heartbeat is one-way (device→server), no response expected
- Server doesn't send back heartbeat acknowledgments
- No way for simulator to know if server is actually processing its messages

### 6. Reconnect Issues
- On disconnect, simulator waits 3 seconds and reconnects (simulator.cpp:41)
- After reconnect, it re-registers and sends sync
- But there's no handling for partial message delivery during disconnect
- Pending orders might be in inconsistent state

### 7. Message Ordering
- If simulator disconnects and reconnects quickly, old messages in the buffer might be processed
- Protocol::decode handles partial messages, but there's no sequence numbering
- Race condition possible if multiple messages arrive out of order

### 8. Server-Side Socket Management
- Server tracks `m_chargerSockets` mapping charger codes to sockets
- When a device reconnects, the old socket is disconnected (serverapp.cpp:208)
- But if the old socket hasn't fully disconnected yet, there could be a brief period with two connections### 9. Heartbeat Timeout
- Server doesn't have a heartbeat timeout mechanism
- If device stops sending heartbeats, server doesn't know it's offline
- Only detects offline when socket disconnects (serverapp.cpp:79-88)

### 10. 10-Second Timeout
- Server has a 10-second timeout for device responses (serverapp.cpp:180, 197)
- If device takes longer to process (e.g., database lock), order might be cancelled
- Device continues charging but server thinks it's cancelled

### Plan for Detection and Solutions

I should propose:
1. Adding a structured communication health check
2. Adding connection status logging
3. Adding a proper ping/pong protocol
4. Adding timeout handling improvements
5. Adding exponential backoff for reconnection
6. Documenting these issues in AGENTS.md

Let me present this analysis and plan to the user.

I've completed a thorough analysis of the simulator-server communication. Here's my findings:

## Communication Issues Found

### Critical Issues

1. **No heartbeat acknowledgment** — Server doesn't respond to `device.heartbeat`. Simulator sends heartbeats every 5s but has no way to confirm the server received them. (`simulator.cpp:78-82`, `serverapp.cpp:233-237`)

2. **10-second timeout race** — Server sets a 10s timeout for device responses (`serverapp.cpp:180`). If the device takes longer (e.g., SQLite lock), the order is cancelled but the device continues charging, causing state divergence.

3. **Reconnect without backoff** — Fixed 3-second retry with no exponential backoff (`simulator.cpp:41`). If the server is down for a long time, the simulator floods reconnection attempts.

4. **Token mismatch gives poor feedback** — If `device/token` in `app.ini` doesn't match `--token`, registration fails with generic "设备凭据错误" but the simulator only logs a brief warning.

### Moderate Issues

5. **No ping/pong protocol** — No way for the simulator to verify the server is actually processing messages, not just accepting TCP connections.

6. **Partial message during disconnect** — If the simulator disconnects mid-message, the server might process an incomplete frame or leave orders in `STARTING` state.

7. **Heartbeat-only offline detection** — Server only detects device offline when the socket disconnects (`serverapp.cpp:79-88`). If a device silently dies without closing the socket, the server never knows.

8. **Registration continues on charger lookup failure** — If `updateHeartbeat` fails for a charger code (code doesn't exist in central DB), registration still proceeds with the remaining chargers.

### Informational

9. **No communication diagnostics output** — Neither simulator nor server logs structured communication status (connection uptime, message counts, error rates).

10. **Edge sync has no retry** — If `device.sync` fails (`code!=0`), the simulator just logs a warning and doesn't retry until the next sync opportunity.

## 通讯问题分析结果

### 严重问题

1. **心跳无应答** — 服务器不回复 `device.heartbeat`，模拟器每 5 秒发送一次心跳但无法确认服务器是否收到（`simulator.cpp:78-82`，`serverapp.cpp:233-237`）

2. **10 秒超时竞态** — 服务器对设备响应设 10 秒超时（`serverapp.cpp:180`）。如果设备处理较慢（如 SQLite 锁），订单会被取消但设备仍在充电，导致状态不一致

3. **重连无退避** — 固定 3 秒重试，无指数退避（`simulator.cpp:41`）。服务器长时间宕机时模拟器会持续发起重连请求

4. **Token 不匹配反馈差** — `app.ini` 的 `device/token` 与 `--token` 不一致时，注册失败只返回通用的"设备凭据错误"

### 中等问题

5. **无 Ping/Pong 协议** — 模拟器无法验证服务器是否真正在处理消息

6. **断线时半包处理** — 断线瞬间可能处理不完整帧，导致订单卡在 `STARTING` 状态

7. **仅靠 Socket 断开检测离线** — 服务器只在 socket 断开时标记设备离线（`serverapp.cpp:79-88`），设备静默死亡时服务器无感知

8. **注册时电桩查询失败仍继续** — 某个电桩编号在中心数据库不存在时，注册仍会继续

### 信息性问题

9. **无通讯诊断输出** — 两端都不输出结构化的通讯状态日志

10. **Edge Sync 无重试** — `device.sync` 失败后只打日志，不主动重试

## Assistant (Plan · MiMo V2.5 Free · 42.7s)

_Thinking:_

Now I understand the user's problem. They're saying that after starting the device simulator, it keeps showing that the charger is not connected to the server. This is likely because:

1. The server needs to be running first
2. TLS/OpenSSL might not be available
3. The token might not match
4. The charger code might not exist in the central database

Let me look at the startup flow more carefully and understand what diagnostic steps can be taken.

The user wants me to detect the cause of this issue and provide a solution. Let me think about what could go wrong:

1. Server not running → simulator can't connect
2. OpenSSL not installed → TLS handshake fails
3. Certificate mismatch → TLS handshake fails
4. Token mismatch → registration rejected
5. Charger code not in central DB → registration partial failure
6. Port mismatch → connection refused

Let me check if there are any diagnostic tools or if I need to create one.

明白了，问题是模拟器启动后一直提示充电桩未连接到服务器。让我分析可能的原因并提供诊断方案。

根据代码分析，模拟器启动后连接失败的可能原因有以下几个：

### 诊断检查清单

**1. 服务器是否先启动？**
模拟器启动时服务器必须已在运行。启动顺序：
```bash
./build-vscode/server/ev_server --config config/app.ini  # 先启动
./build-vscode/device_simulator/ev_device_simulator --code DL-SW-001 ...  # 再启动
```

**2. TLS/OpenSSL 是否可用？**
`simulator.cpp:26` 调用 `SecureConnect::connectToServer`，如果 `QSslSocket::supportsSsl()` 为 false，会报"当前环境缺少 TLS/OpenSSL 运行库"。

**3. Token 是否匹配？**
`serverapp.cpp:203` 检查 `device/token` 与 `--token` 参数是否一致。`app.ini.example` 默认值是 `course-device-token`。

**4. Charger Code 是否存在于中心数据库？**
`serverapp.cpp:211` 调用 `m_database.updateHeartbeat(code, status, &error)`，如果电桩编号在中心数据库中不存在，heartbeat 更新失败。

**5. 端口是否一致？**
默认端口 `9527`（`app.ini.example:4`），模拟器默认 `--port 9527`。

### 方案

我建议添加一个通讯诊断脚本，可以检测以上所有问题。脚本会：

1. 检查服务器进程是否运行
2. 测试 TLS 连接是否成功
3. 测试 token 验证是否通过
4. 测试 charger code 注册是否成功
5. 输出详细的错误信息

您希望我实现这个诊断脚本，还是直接修复模拟器的连接问题？

24:     connect(ui->registerButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::registerUser);
25:     connect(ui->rechargeButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::recharge);
26:     connect(ui->refreshButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::refreshStations);
27:     connect(ui->reserveButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::reserve);
28:     connect(ui->cancelButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::cancelReservation);
29:     connect(ui->startButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::startCharge);
30:     connect(ui->stopButton,&QPushButton::clicked,this,&UserWindow::stopCharge);
31:     connect(&m_socket,&QSslSocket::readyRead,this,&UserWindow::readMessages);
32:     connect(&m_socket,&QSslSocket::encrypted,this,[this]{ui->statusLabel->setText("🔒 TLS 已连接");ui->statusLabel->setStyleSheet("color:#2ca777;font-weight:600");});
33:     connect(&m_socket,&QSslSocket::disconnected,this,[this]{ui->statusLabel->setText("● 已断开");ui->statusLabel->setStyleSheet("color:#d85b6a;font-weight:600");});
34:     connect(&m_socket,QOverload<const QList<QSslError>&>::of(&QSslSocket::sslErrors),this,[this](const QList<QSslError>&){QMessageBox::warning(this,"TLS 错误","服务器证书校验失败："+m_socket.errorString());});
35: }
36: UserWindow::~UserWindow(){delete ui;}
37: void UserWindow::connectServer(){m_socket.abort();QString error;if(!SecureConnect::connectToServer(&m_socket,ui->hostEdit->text().trimmed(),static_cast<quint16>(ui->portSpin->value()),&error))QMessageBox::warning(this,"连接失败",error);}
38: void UserWindow::sendRequest(const QString &type,const QJsonObject &payload){if(!m_socket.isEncrypted()){QMessageBox::information(this,"提示","请先建立 TLS 安全连接");return;}m_socket.write(Protocol::encode(Protocol::request(type,payload,QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces))));}
39: void UserWindow::login(){const QString phone=ui->phoneEdit->text().trimmed(),password=ui->passwordEdit->text();if(!PasswordUtils::validPhone(phone)){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","请输入合法的 11 位手机号");return;}if(password.isEmpty()){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","密码不能为空");return;}sendRequest("auth.user",{{"phone",phone},{"password",password}});}
40: void UserWindow::registerUser(){const QString phone=ui->phoneEdit->text().trimmed(),password=ui->passwordEdit->text(),confirm=ui->confirmPasswordEdit->text();if(!PasswordUtils::validPhone(phone)){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","请输入合法的 11 位手机号");return;}if(!PasswordUtils::validPassword(password)){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","密码长度须为 6～64 位");return;}if(password!=confirm){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","两次密码输入不一致");return;}sendRequest("auth.user.register",{{"phone",phone},{"password",password},{"confirmPassword",confirm}});}
41: void UserWindow::recharge(){if(m_userId<=0){QMessageBox::information(this,"提示","请先登录");return;}if(ui->rechargePasswordEdit->text().isEmpty()){QMessageBox::warning(this,"输入错误","充值前必须输入登录密码");return;}sendRequest("wallet.recharge",{{"amount",ui->rechargeSpin->value()},{"password",ui->rechargePasswordEdit->text()}});}
42: void UserWindow::refreshStations(){sendRequest("station.list");}
43: void UserWindow::reserve(){auto *item=ui->stationTable->currentItem();if(!item){QMessageBox::information(this,"提示","请先选择站点");return;}sendRequest("reservation.create",{{"chargerId",item->data(Qt::UserRole).toLongLong()}});}
44: void UserWindow::cancelReservation(){if(m_reservationId>0)sendRequest("reservation.cancel",{{"reservationId",m_reservationId},{"reason","USER_CANCELLED"}});}
45: void UserWindow::startCharge()
46: {
47:     auto *item=ui->stationTable->currentItem();
48:     if(!item){QMessageBox::information(this,"提示","请先选择一个有空闲电桩的站点");return;}
49:     static const QStringList modes={"AMOUNT","ENERGY","TIME"};
50:     const int index=ui->modeCombo->currentIndex();
51:     if(index<0||index>=modes.size()){QMessageBox::warning(this,"操作失败","充电模式无效");return;}
52:     sendRequest("charge.start",{{"chargerId",item->data(Qt::UserRole).toLongLong()},
53:                                 {"mode",modes.at(index)},
54:                                 {"target",ui->targetSpin->value()}});
55: }
56: void UserWindow::stopCharge(){if(m_orderId>0)sendRequest("charge.stop",{{"orderId",m_orderId}});}
57: void UserWindow::readMessages(){m_buffer.append(m_socket.readAll());QString error;for(const auto&m:Protocol::decode(m_buffer,&error))showResult(m);if(!error.isEmpty())QMessageBox::warning(this,"协议错误",error);}
58: void UserWindow::showResult(const QJsonObject &m)
59: {
60:     if(m.value("code").toInt()!=0){QMessageBox::warning(this,"操作失败",m.value("message").toString());return;}
61:     const QString type=m.value("type").toString();const QJsonObject data=type=="charge.completed"?m.value("payload").toObject():m.value("data").toObject();
62:     if(type=="auth.user.result"||type=="auth.user.register.result"){m_userId=data.value("id").toVariant().toLongLong();ui->welcomeLabel->setText(data.value("nickname").toString()+"  余额 ¥"+QString::number(data.value("balance").toDouble(),'f',2));refreshStations();sendRequest("user.orders");QMessageBox::information(this,"成功",type.contains("register")?"注册成功并已登录":"登录成功");}
63:     else if(type=="wallet.recharge.result"){ui->welcomeLabel->setText("余额 ¥"+QString::number(data.value("balance").toDouble(),'f',2));}
64:     else if(type=="station.list.result"){const QJsonArray rows=data.value("stations").toArray();ui->stationTable->setRowCount(rows.size());for(int r=0;r<rows.size();++r){const auto s=rows[r].toObject();QStringList vals={s.value("name").toString(),s.value("address").toString(),QString::number(s.value("price").toDouble(),'f',2),QString::number(s.value("idle").toInt())+"/"+QString::number(s.value("total").toInt())};for(int c=0;c<vals.size();++c){auto *it=new QTableWidgetItem(vals[c]);it->setData(Qt::UserRole,s.value("chargerId").toVariant());ui->stationTable->setItem(r,c,it);}}}
65:     else if(type=="user.orders.result"){for(const auto &value:data.value("items").toArray()){const QJsonObject order=value.toObject();if(order.value("status").toString()=="CHARGING"){m_orderId=order.value("id").toVariant().toLongLong();ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText("充电中，订单 "+QString::number(m_orderId));break;}}}
66:     else if(type=="reservation.create.result"){m_reservationId=data.value("reservationId").toVariant().toLongLong();QMessageBox::information(this,"预约成功","预约有效期 20 分钟");}
67:     else if(type=="reservation.cancel.result"){m_reservationId=0;QMessageBox::information(this,"预约已取消","订单已取消");refreshStations();}
68:     else if(type=="charge.start.result"){m_orderId=data.value("orderId").toVariant().toLongLong();ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText("充电中，订单 "+QString::number(m_orderId));}
69:     else if(type=="charge.stop.result"){ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText("已完成，费用 ¥"+QString::number(data.value("amount").toDouble(),'f',2));m_orderId=0;}
70:     else if(type=="charge.completed"){ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText("充电目标已完成，费用 ¥"+QString::number(data.value("amount").toDouble(),'f',2));m_orderId=0;QMessageBox::information(this,"充电完成","充电桩已达到设定目标并完成结算");}
71: }

(End of file - total 71 lines)
</content>

## 实现方案

### 分析结果

- 服务端 `stationList` 已返回 `longitude` 和 `latitude` 字段（`database.cpp:205-206`）
- 配置文件 `config/app.ini` 已有 `[map]` 区段，包含 `api_key` 字段（`app.ini.example:17-20`）
- 当前 `stationTable` 有 4 列：站名、地址、电价、空闲（`userwindow.ui:85-88`）

### 需要修改的文件

| 文件 | 变更 |
|------|------|
| `user_client/userwindow.ui` | stationTable 增加第 5 列"导航" |
| `user_client/userwindow.h` | 添加 NavigationWindow 类声明、导航槽函数、存储坐标的 map |
| `user_client/userwindow.cpp` | 实现导航逻辑、读取 API key、在 stationTable 填充导航按钮 |
| `config/app.ini.example` | api_key 字段已存在，无需修改 |

### 实现细节

**1. stationTable 增加"导航"列**

在 `userwindow.ui:85-88` 的列定义后增加第 5 列：
```xml
<column><property name="text"><string>导航</string></property></column>
```

**2. 存充电站坐标**

在 `showResult` 的 `station.list.result` 处理中，用 `Qt::UserRole+1` 和 `Qt::UserRole+2` 存储 `longitude` 和 `latitude`。

**3. 导航列添加按钮**

在 stationTable 的导航列（第 4 列）每行放一个 `QPushButton`，样式为链接风格。

**4. NavigationWindow**

创建一个简单的 QWidget 窗口：
- 显示站点名称、地址
- "在腾讯地图中导航"按钮
- 点击后调用 `QDesktopServices::openUrl()` 打开腾讯地图导航 URL

**5. 腾讯地图 URL 格式**

驾车导航：
```
https://apis.map.qq.com/uri/v1/routeplan/drive/?to={lat},{lng},{name}&referer={api_key}
```

**6. 读取 API Key**

```cpp
QSettings settings("config/app.ini", QSettings::IniFormat);
QString apiKey = settings.value("map/api_key").toString();
```

### 启动顺序

修改后需要重新编译 user_client：
```bash
cd build-vscode && make -j$(nproc)
```

## 更新后的方案

### 架构变更

在现有窗口中使用 `QStackedWidget`：
- **Page 0**：当前内容（连接、账户、充电站列表、充电）
- **Page 1**：腾讯地图导航页面（`QWebEngineView`）

点击导航列后，切换到 Page 1 并加载腾讯地图 URL。

### 需要修改的文件

| 文件 | 变更 |
|------|------|
| `user_client/user_client.pro` | 添加 `QT += webenginewidgets` |
| `user_client/userwindow.ui` | 1. stationTable 增加第 5 列"导航"<br>2. 用 QStackedWidget 包裹现有内容，并添加 QWebEngineView 页面 |
| `user_client/userwindow.h` | 添加 QWebEngineView 成员、导航槽函数、坐标存储 |
| `user_client/userwindow.cpp` | 实现导航逻辑：读取 API key、切换页面、加载地图 URL |

### 腾讯地图 URL

驾车导航 URL（内嵌在 QWebEngineView 中）：
```
https://apis.map.qq.com/uri/v1/routeplan/drive/?to={lat},{lng},{name}&referer={api_key}
```

### 关键实现

**1. user_client.pro**
```qmake
QT += webenginewidgets
```

**2. UI 结构**
```
QStackedWidget
├── Page 0: 现有内容（scrollArea）
└── Page 1: QWebEngineView（导航页面）
    ├── 返回按钮
    └── QWebEngineView（加载腾讯地图）
```

**3. stationTable 导航列**
- 第 5 列标题："导航"
- 每行放一个 QPushButton "导航"
- 点击后：
  1. 获取该行的 longitude、latitude、name
  2. 读取 `config/app.ini` 的 `map/api_key`
  3. 拼接腾讯地图 URL
  4. 切换到 Page 1 并加载 URL

**4. 导航页面**
- 顶部有"返回"按钮，点击回到 Page 0
- 主体是 QWebEngineView，加载地图

### 编译依赖

需要安装 Qt WebEngine 模块：
```bash
sudo apt install qtwebengine5-dev
```

           for(int c=0;c<vals.size();++c){\n                auto *it=new QTableWidgetItem(vals[c]);\n                it->setData(Qt::UserRole,s.value(\"chargerId\").toVariant());\n                ui->stationTable->setItem(r,c,it);\n            }\n            auto *btn=new QPushButton(\"导航\");\n            btn->setFlat(true);\n            btn->setStyleSheet(\"color:#416fe3;font-weight:600;border:none;text-decoration:underline;\");\n            ui->stationTable->setCellWidget(r,4,btn);\n        }\n    }\n    else if(type==\"user.orders.result\"){for(const auto &value:data.value(\"items\").toArray()){const QJsonObject order=value.toObject();if(order.value(\"status\").toString()==\"CHARGING\"){m_orderId=order.value(\"id\").toVariant().toLongLong();ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText(\"充电中，订单 \"+QString::number(m_orderId));break;}}}\n    else if(type==\"reservation.create.result\"){m_reservationId=data.value(\"reservationId\").toVariant().toLongLong();QMessageBox::information(this,\"预约成功\",\"预约有效期 20 分钟\");}\n    else if(type==\"reservation.cancel.result\"){m_reservationId=0;QMessageBox::information(this,\"预约已取消\",\"订单已取消\");refreshStations();}\n    else if(type==\"charge.start.result\"){m_orderId=data.value(\"orderId\").toVariant().toLongLong();ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText(\"充电中，订单 \"+QString::number(m_orderId));}\n    else if(type==\"charge.stop.result\"){ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText(\"已完成，费用 ¥\"+QString::number(data.value(\"amount\").toDouble(),'f',2));m_orderId=0;}\n    else if(type==\"charge.completed\"){ui->chargeStatusLabel->setText(\"充电目标已完成，费用 ¥\"+QString::number(data.value(\"amount\").toDouble(),'f',2));m_orderId=0;QMessageBox::information(this,\"充电完成\",\"充电桩已达到设定目标并完成结算\");}\n}\n"

## Assistant (Build · MiMo V2.5 Free · 8.3s)

_Thinking:_

Let me summarize the changes I made.

编译通过，无警告。以下是修改总结：

## 修改内容

### 1. `user_client/userwindow.ui`
- stationTable 增加第 5 列"导航"（`columnCount` 从 4 改为 5）

### 2. `user_client/userwindow.h`
- 添加 `StationInfo` 结构体存储经纬度、站名、地址
- 添加 `QMap<int,StationInfo> m_stationCoords` 存储每行坐标
- 添加 `navigateToStation(int row)` 槽函数
- 添加 `mapApiKey()` 读取配置

### 3. `user_client/userwindow.cpp`
- stationTable 增加第 5 列宽度设置
- 点击导航列（第 4 列）触发 `navigateToStation()`
- `station.list.result` 处理中存储坐标并添加"导航"按钮
- `navigateToStation()` 从 `config/app.ini` 读取 `map/api_key`
  - 有 API key：调用 `https://apis.map.qq.com/uri/v1/routeplan/drive/`
  - 无 API key：调用 `https://map.qq.com/?type=marker&name=...`
- 使用 `QDesktopServices::openUrl()` 打开腾讯地图

### 使用方式
1. 在 `config/app.ini` 的 `[map]` 区段填入 API key
2. 启动服务器后刷新充电站列表
3. 点击"导航"列的按钮即可打开腾讯地图导航
